        bool elig = not z.signaled and z.tier <= minTier and (not fvgFillDead or z.state < 3) and z.srcPoolId != 0 and not na(z.srcMssBar) and z.tap50Bar >= 0 and bar_index - z.tap50Bar <= confirmWin
        int  retQ = fill >= 0.8 ? 3 : fill >= 0.5 ? 2 : 1
        if sup
            if elig and zoneBearConf and high >= bot and (not sdCandShort or z.tier < sdTierS)
                sdCandShort := true
                sdZoneS     := z
                sdTopShort  := top
                sdBotShort  := bot
                sdTierS     := z.tier
                sdRetQS     := retQ
        else
            if elig and zoneBullConf and low <= top and (not sdCandLong or z.tier < sdTierL)
                sdCandLong  := true
                sdZoneL     := z
                sdTopLong   := top
                sdBotLong   := bot
                sdTierL     := z.tier
                sdRetQL     := retQ

// ---- create new zones (displacement + FVG) ----------------------------------
bullFVG = low > high[2]
bearFVG = high < low[2]
zDispUp = bullBar[1] and body[1] >= dispFactor * atr
zDispDn = bearBar[1] and body[1] >= dispFactor * atr
// V7.5 · §13 · an FVG ZONE needs the same minimum height as the execution gap
newDemand = bullFVG and zDispUp and low - high[2] >= fvgMinAtr * atr
newSupply = bearFVG and zDispDn and low[2] - high >= fvgMinAtr * atr

// V7.5 · RECLAIM R1 · the demand and supply creation blocks were mirror images
// (same overlap / BPR / dedupe loop, same OB search, same provenance stamp) and
// are ONE function now, called once per side. Byte-identical decisions.
f_mkZone(bool isSupply) =>
    float zTop = isSupply ? math.min(low[2], high + maxZoneAtr * atr) : low
    float zBot = isSupply ? high : math.max(high[2], low - maxZoneAtr * atr)
    [tZ, rpZone, smZ, mchZ] = f_gradeZone(isSupply, zTop, zBot)
    bool liqZ = not na(rpZone)
    // ═══ V7.7 · F-03 · DECIDE FIRST, DELETE SECOND ═══════════════════════════════
    //   v7.6 ran one pass that both set `dup` and deleted weaker overlapping zones.
    //   When a new gap overlapped SEVERAL same-side zones (routine in a compressed
    //   range) the descending walk could delete a weaker one at a high index and only
    //   THEN meet a stronger one at a low index that set `dup` — so the replacement
    //   was never built and a live POI had been destroyed for nothing. The two jobs
    //   are separate passes now: pass 1 answers "is this gap redundant?", and the
    //   deletions in pass 2 only happen once we know a replacement is actually coming.
    //   A zone a live setup is standing on is never removed either (F-02).
    bool dup = false
    if array.size(zones) > 0
        // ---- pass 1 · the verdict, plus the BPR re-tag (which deletes nothing)
        for i = 0 to array.size(zones) - 1
            Zone z = array.get(zones, i)
            bool ov = not (zBot > z.top or zTop < z.bot)
            // §17 · BPR: this gap opened inside a LIVE gap of the opposite polarity.
            // The overlap is a balanced price range — both sides delivered there and
            // neither held. It is a PD array, so the surviving zone is re-tagged.
            // V7.5 · it is NOT promoted any more: a tier is causal evidence and an
            // unrelated later gap is not part of this zone's chain (§16).
            if ov and showBPR and z.isSupply != isSupply and z.isFvg and z.state < 3 and not z.flipped
                z.pa      := PA_BPR
                z.kindTxt := "BPR"
                box.set_text(z.bx, "BPR " + f_tierTxt(z.tier))
            // BUG-21: a SPENT zone is never replaced by a fresh overlapping
            // one — that re-armed the same price for a second entry.
            // V7.7 · a BOUND zone counts as a survivor too: the new gap may not
            //   evict the array a setup is mid-narrative on.
            if z.isSupply == isSupply and ov and (z.signaled or z.bound != 0 or z.tier <= tZ)
                dup := true
        // ---- pass 2 · only now, and only if a replacement is coming
        if not dup
            for i = array.size(zones) - 1 to 0
                Zone z = array.get(zones, i)
                if z.isSupply == isSupply and not (zBot > z.top or zTop < z.bot)
                    box.delete(z.bx)
                    line.delete(z.mid)
                    array.remove(zones, i)
    if not dup
        int dq = isSupply ? dqDn[1] : dqUp[1]
        Zone zn = f_newZone(isSupply, zTop, zBot, tZ, "", 0, true)
        f_zoneProv(zn, isSupply, rpZone, liqZ, smZ, mchZ, dq)
        array.push(zones, zn)
        // §14 · THE ORDER BLOCK OF THIS LEG, and only of this leg. The OB is the
        // last opposing candle before the displacement that just left an FVG —
        // so it exists only where structure and imbalance already agree, which
        // is what stops "every opposite-coloured candle is an OB". It carries
        // the SAME provenance as the gap (V7.5: stamped by the same helper).
        if showOB and tZ <= 2
            bool  o2  = isSupply ? bullBar[2] : bearBar[2]
            bool  o3  = isSupply ? bullBar[3] : bearBar[3]
            bool  o4  = isSupply ? bullBar[4] : bearBar[4]
            int   obI = o2 ? 2 : o3 ? 3 : o4 ? 4 : -1
            float obT = o2 ? high[2] : o3 ? high[3] : o4 ? high[4] : na
            float obB = o2 ? low[2]  : o3 ? low[3]  : o4 ? low[4]  : na
            if obI > 0 and obT - obB <= maxZoneAtr * atr
                // V7.6 · F-03 · THE OB IS A DIFFERENT BOX AND HAS TO PROVE IT ITSELF.
                //   `liqZ` means "the raid wick sits at THIS array's origin", and it was
                //   evaluated against the GAP's coordinates. The OB is one to three
                //   candles earlier at a different price band, so handing it liqZ is
                //   provenance by assertion — the exact failure this module's header
                //   names. The containment test is re-run on the OB's own edges. A
                //   failure costs it the stamp and caps it at T2; the array is still
                //   drawn and is still machine-bindable, it simply owns no chain.
                //   V7.7 F-05 · the wick tested is the one the ZONE was graded on
                //   (rpZone), not "the freshest raid on the side" any more.
                float rpxZ = na
                if liqZ
                    rpxZ := rpZone.raidExt
                bool  liqO = liqZ and not na(rpxZ) and
                     (isSupply ? rpxZ <= obT + sdRaidAtr * atr and rpxZ >= obB - poiBindAtr * atr
                     : rpxZ <= obT + poiBindAtr * atr and rpxZ >= obB - sdRaidAtr * atr)
                int   tO   = liqO ? tZ : math.max(tZ, 2)
                Zone ob = f_newZone(isSupply, obT, obB, tO, isSupply ? "OB ▼" : "OB ▲", bar_index - obI, false)
                ob.pa := PA_OB
                f_zoneProv(ob, isSupply, rpZone, liqO, smZ, mchZ, dq)
                array.push(zones, ob)
    true
if newDemand and confirmed
    f_mkZone(false)
if newSupply and confirmed
    f_mkZone(true)

// ---- rejection blocks: swing-point wick POIs --------------------------------
// The pivot must have SWEPT liquidity and price must have DISPLACED away.
rbSwpH = high[pivLen] > ta.highest(high, pivLen * 2)[pivLen + 1]
rbDspH = ta.lowest(low, pivLen) <= high[pivLen] - dispFactor * atr
rbSwpL = low[pivLen] < ta.lowest(low, pivLen * 2)[pivLen + 1]
rbDspL = ta.highest(high, pivLen) >= low[pivLen] + dispFactor * atr
if showRB and confirmed
    if not na(ph)
        float rbTop = high[pivLen]
        float rbBot = math.max(open[pivLen], close[pivLen])
        if rbTop - rbBot >= minWickAtr * atr and rbSwpH and rbDspH
            bool rbDup = false
            if array.size(zones) > 0
                for i = array.size(zones) - 1 to 0
                    Zone z = array.get(zones, i)
                    if z.isSupply and not (rbBot > z.top or rbTop < z.bot)
                        rbDup := true
                        break
            if not rbDup
                Zone rz = f_newZone(true, rbTop, rbBot, structDir <= 0 ? 2 : 3, "REJECTION ▼", bar_index - pivLen, false)
                rz.pa := PA_REJ
                array.push(zones, rz)
    if not na(pl)
        float rbBot2 = low[pivLen]
        float rbTop2 = math.min(open[pivLen], close[pivLen])
        if rbTop2 - rbBot2 >= minWickAtr * atr and rbSwpL and rbDspL
            bool rbDup2 = false
            if array.size(zones) > 0
                for i = array.size(zones) - 1 to 0
                    Zone z = array.get(zones, i)
                    if not z.isSupply and not (rbBot2 > z.top or rbTop2 < z.bot)
                        rbDup2 := true
                        break
            if not rbDup2
                Zone rz2 = f_newZone(false, rbTop2, rbBot2, structDir >= 0 ? 2 : 3, "REJECTION ▲", bar_index - pivLen, false)
                rz2.pa := PA_REJ
                array.push(zones, rz2)

// ---- NEXT eligible buy / sell POI -------------------------------------------
float nbTop = na
float nbBot = na
float nsTop = na
float nsBot = na
int   nbTier  = 3
int   nsTier  = 3
if array.size(zones) > 0
    for i = 0 to array.size(zones) - 1
        Zone z = array.get(zones, i)
        float t = z.top             // V5.4-D1
        float b = z.bot
        bool  ok = not z.signaled and (not fvgFillDead or z.state < 3)
        if ok and not z.isSupply and b <= close and (na(nbTop) or t > nbTop)
            nbTop   := t
            nbBot   := b
            nbTier  := z.tier
        if ok and z.isSupply and t >= close and (na(nsBot) or b < nsBot)
            nsTop   := t
            nsBot   := b
            nsTier  := z.tier

touchNextBuy  = confirmed and not na(nbTop) and not na(nbTop[1]) and low <= nbTop and low[1] > nbTop[1]
touchNextSell = confirmed and not na(nsBot) and not na(nsBot[1]) and high >= nsBot and high[1] < nsBot[1]

// ═══════════════════════════ MODULE 11 · PATTERN PRODUCERS (candidates only)
// Everything in this module produces a CANDIDATE. None of it can print an
// entry and none of it writes a final stop. V5.3-C3, stated honestly: the
// trap watch DOES latch wFired on its own candidate — one candidate per
// armed watch, the anti-duplicate choice, even if the TRADE ENGINE then
// blocks that candidate. Everything else (cooldowns, zone.signaled,
// pool.spent) is consumed by the TRADE ENGINE, and only if it emits (BUG-5).
// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

// ═══════════ V5.5-E6 · TRAP ENGINE — FAILED BREAKOUT → RECLAIM (§15 / §16)
// This is v5.4's ① / ② breakout watch, promoted from "a price-action pattern"
// to the file's TRAP engine, because that is what it already was: a structural
// break, then late entrants, then failure, then a reclaim through the level.
// Three things are new.
//
//   1 · THE BROKEN LEVEL MUST BE CROWDED LIQUIDITY. §16 separates a trap from a
//       plain failed breakout by exactly this: the liquidity taken has to be
//       obvious. `trapNeedPool` requires a POOL IN THE REGISTRY to have been
//       BROKEN on the arming bar, not merely a pivot violated.
//   2 · THE TRAP IS GRADED. §15 lists the evidence; five of those items are
//       measurable here and f_trapQual scores them A / B / C. v5.4 graded every
//       failed breakout identically, which is precisely what §15 forbids.
//   3 · IT IS A DESCRIPTOR FIRST. A confirmed trap is written onto the liquidity
//       event (f_evTag) and sets the producer's POI tier. It pays NO liquidity
//       points — the raid it describes already paid them once (§8).
//
// ② (shrinking wicks = absorption) was RETIRED in v7.4 (reclaim R1): dead at
// the default minTier since v5.3-C2, no stop, no grade. AUDIT-v7.3-FORENSIC.md §5.
f_trapQual(bool pooled, float pen, int bars, bool decisive, bool disp) =>
    int pts = (pooled ? 1 : 0) + (pen <= 1.0 ? 1 : 0) + (bars <= trapReclMax ? 1 : 0) +
         (decisive ? 1 : 0) + (disp ? 1 : 0)
    pts >= 4 ? RQ_A : pts >= 3 ? RQ_B : RQ_C

// V7.1 · §15 / §16 · AN ELITE TRAP. v7.0 capped every trap at tier 2 and gated
// the producer behind "All engines", so the one pattern ICT treats as a primary
// entry model could never clear minTier = 2 into the A rung and never existed at
// all in the two stricter modes. An ELITE trap is ALL FIVE ingredients at once:
// tracked liquidity was broken, the overshoot stayed shallow, the reclaim came
// inside trapReclMax bars, the reclaim close was decisive, and it displaced.
// That is a complete causal chain — raid, failure, reclaim, delivery — so it
// earns a tier-1 POI and, with a grade-A raid behind it, the A+ rung.
f_trapElite(bool pooled, float pen, int bars, bool decisive, bool disp) =>
    trapElite and pooled and pen <= 1.0 and bars <= trapReclMax and decisive and disp

// ═══ V7.6 · F-08 · ONE WATCH PER SIDE ════════════════════════════════════════
// v7.5 held the trap in a single scalar `wState` (0 idle · 1 broke up · -1 broke
// down). An upward break therefore OCCUPIED the engine: until maxAfterBrk bars had
// passed, a runaway killed it, or it fired, no downward break could arm a watch at
// all. For a pattern v7.1 deliberately promoted to tier-1 / A+ eligible, a shared
// slot is an arbitrary constraint — the two sides describe different liquidity and
// have no reason to compete. The state is a UDT now, instantiated twice.
//
// The wick memory is four SCALARS rather than an array: the growth tests only ever
// read the last four, and two live instances of `array.new<float>()` plus their
// push / size / get calls cost more than four field writes.
//
// `up` = this watch tracks an UPWARD break, so its failure is a BEAR TRAP and its
// signal is a SHORT. Every threshold, grade and expiry rule is v7.5's, unchanged.
type Trap
    int   st    = 0        // 0 idle · 1 armed
    float lvl   = na       // the level that was broken
    int   sbar  = na       // arming bar
    float ext   = na       // furthest the breakout ran
    bool  fired = false    // one candidate per armed watch (V5.3-C3)
    bool  pooled = false   // V5.5-E6 · a TRACKED pool broke on the arming bar
    int   knd   = -1       // P1-4 · class of that pool
    int   id    = 0        // V7.4 F-04 · its identity and price
    float px    = na
    float w1    = na       // wick ring, newest first
    float w2    = na
    float w3    = na
    float w4    = na

f_trapAdv(Trap t, bool up) =>
    bool  fire = false
    float psl  = na
    int   q    = RQ_NONE
    bool  el   = false
    if confirmed
        bool  brk = up ? brokeUp : brokeDn
        float blv = up ? brkUpLvl : brkDnLvl
        bool  tpl = up ? tPoolU : tPoolD
        // arm only when idle, and only off a STRUCTURAL break (a weak run no longer
        // arms a trap watch — BUG-2 made every weak close-through arm one). V5.5-E6
        // adds the crowded-liquidity requirement.
        if t.st == 0 and brk and body >= BRK_BODY_ATR * atr and not na(blv) and (not trapNeedPool or tpl)
            t.st     := 1
            t.lvl    := blv
            t.sbar   := bar_index
            t.ext    := up ? high : low
            t.fired  := false
            t.pooled := tpl
            t.knd    := tpl ? (up ? brkUpKind : brkDnKind) : -1
            t.id     := tpl ? (up ? brkUpId : brkDnId) : 0
            t.px     := tpl ? (up ? brkUpPx : brkDnPx) : na
            t.w1     := na
            t.w2     := na
            t.w3     := na
            t.w4     := na
        if t.st != 0
            t.w4  := t.w3
            t.w3  := t.w2
            t.w2  := t.w1
            t.w1  := up ? upWick : dnWick
            t.ext := up ? math.max(t.ext, high) : math.min(t.ext, low)
            bool grow = not na(t.w3) and t.w1 > t.w2 and t.w2 > t.w3 and t.w2 >= minWickAtr * atr
            bool grew = not na(t.w4) and t.w2 > t.w3 and t.w3 > t.w4 and t.w3 >= minWickAtr * atr
            bool recl = up ? bearBar and close < t.lvl : bullBar and close > t.lvl
            if not t.fired and showS1 and (grow or grew) and body >= BRK_BODY_ATR * atr and recl
                float pen  = math.max(up ? t.ext - t.lvl : t.lvl - t.ext, 0) / atr
                int   bars = bar_index - t.sbar
                bool  dec  = up ? close < t.lvl - NEAR_LVL_ATR * atr : close > t.lvl + NEAR_LVL_ATR * atr
                bool  dsp  = up ? dispDnBar : dispUpBar
                fire    := true
                psl     := t.ext
                q       := f_trapQual(t.pooled, pen, bars, dec, dsp)
                el      := f_trapElite(t.pooled, pen, bars, dec, dsp)
                t.fired := true
            // expiry / runaway — the watch dies on its own schedule, not on a signal
            if bar_index - t.sbar > maxAfterBrk
                t.st := 0
            else if up ? close > t.lvl + RUNAWAY_ATR * atr : close < t.lvl - RUNAWAY_ATR * atr
                t.st := 0
    [fire, psl, q, el]

var Trap trapU = Trap.new()     // watches UP breaks   → bear trap → SHORT
var Trap trapD = Trap.new()     // watches DOWN breaks → bull trap → LONG
[pa1Short, paSlShort, trapQS, trapElS] = f_trapAdv(trapU, true)
[pa1Long,  paSlLong,  trapQL, trapElL] = f_trapAdv(trapD, false)

if showS1 and (pa1Long or pa1Short)
    f_chip(pa1Long, (pa1Long ? "BEAR TRAP " : "BULL TRAP ") + f_rqTxt(pa1Long ? trapQL : trapQS),
         color.new(pa1Long ? COL_BULL : COL_BEAR, 45), color.white, mkSize)


// ---- "at the HTF array" location test — moved to MODULE 6 in v7.5 (reused by
//      the HTF context verdict, F-13, instead of being spelled out twice) ------


// ═══════════ V7.0 · CRT AND TURTLE SOUP: REMOVED IN FULL (§0)
// What stood here in v6.4: the `Crt` UDT + f_crtAdvance (a five-stage lifecycle,
// instantiated twice), the `Tbs` UDT + f_tbsAdvance (likewise), their four
// tbsLen rolling-extreme series, the two cooldown pairs, f_ctt_crtCand,
// f_ctt_tbsCand, f_ctt_htfCand, the `Ctt` marking UDT, f_cttTxt, f_cttMark
// (inlined four times), f_cttLvl, f_ctt_chart and f_ctt_htfMark.
//
// All of it is gone, and nothing replaced it in kind. The brief is explicit
// that no entry, score, gate, state transition or confirmation may depend on
// CRT or turtle soup, and the two producers they fed (PR_CRT / PR_TBS) were
// the file's only path from "a three-candle pattern" to an order. The evidence
// they were standing in for — a range extreme taken and reclaimed with
// displacement — is exactly what the liquidity registry's RAID grading plus the
// setup machine's MSS + displacement stages already measure, causally and with
// ownership, which is what §2 asks for and what a pattern match cannot give.
//
// Freed budget paid for: the HTF structure feed above, the order-block and BPR
// PD arrays, the leg-based OTE engine, the inducement test, the trendline
// liquidity engine and the AMD / Judas session layer.


// ══════════════════════ MODULE 11B · LIQUIDITY RUN · CONGESTION · REGIME
// Two engines plus the regime classifier. None is a producer: the run engine is
// a GATE on fading, congestion is a grade modifier / gate, the regime drives the
// execution policy. Nothing here can print a signal.
// V7.5 · TWS (three-wave sequence) REMOVED IN FULL — inputs, Tws UDT,
// f_twsAdvance, f_twsRun, both instances, both chips, the "TWS" descriptor, the
// dashboard row and the alert. It was a descriptor written onto the freshest
// raid on the side (global borrowing) and nothing causal depended on it.
// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════


// ═══════════ MODULE 11C · MTF CRT / TBS / TWS — REMOVED IN v6.3 (TOKEN CAP)
// It was built, it worked as designed, and it measured 8 890 compiled tokens
// against the 516 this file has left (CE10117: 108 630 / 100 256). Four
// request.security calls publishing fourteen values each is 56 HTF series,
// and the feed function is inlined per call site — that is where the cost
// is, not in the state machines. The identical layer, unchanged in every
// rule, runs as 15.crt-tbs-tws-mtf-layer-v1.pine: add it to this chart and
// you have the trade engine here and the 15M / 30M / 1H / 4H structure
// marks there. If you would rather have it inline, the only currency the
// file has left is other systems — the dashboard (≈ 6 090 compiled), the
// debug panel (≈ 3 680) or MODULE 8B's liquidity marking (≈ 3 960).

// ═══════════ V5.5-E4 · LIQUIDITY RUN ENGINE (§11)
// v5.4 had `runUp` / `runDn`: a weak, non-displacement close through ONE level.
// §11 asks for something else entirely — pool A broken, then pool B, then
// expansion, an internal → external progression, and a classification. It also
// states the rule this engine exists to enforce: NEVER fade a liquidity run
// merely because liquidity was taken. A reversal needs exhaustion AND rejection
// AND reclaim AND a structural shift AND displacement.
//
//   RN_UNCONF  one pool broken this direction — nothing established yet.
//   RN_CONT    a second or later pool broken the same way inside runLife bars.
//              This is a RUN, and fading it is what runGate acts on.
//   RN_EXH     the run reached the next pool and RAIDED it instead of breaking
//              it — the first honest sign it is out of fuel.
//   RN_REV     exhaustion, and THEN an opposing shift with displacement. Only
//              here is a fade a trade rather than a hope.
type Run
    int   dir      = 0
    int   cls      = 0
    int   legs     = 0
    int   lastBar  = na
    bool  intToExt = false
    int   exhBar   = na

var Run run = Run.new()
runFresh = false
if confirmed
    if run.dir != 0 and not na(run.lastBar) and bar_index - run.lastBar > runLife
        run.dir      := 0
        run.cls      := RN_NONE
        run.legs     := 0
        run.intToExt := false
        run.exhBar   := na
    // exactly ONE side broke. Both breaking on one candle is not a directional
    // run, it is churn, and the congestion filter is the right reader of that.
    if poolBrkUp != poolBrkDn
        int   nd  = poolBrkUp ? 1 : -1
        int   nk  = poolBrkUp ? brkUpKind : brkDnKind
        bool  ext = nk == PK_EXT or nk == PK_PD or nk == PK_SES or nk == PK_EQ or nk == PK_HTF or nk == PK_PW
        if run.dir == nd and run.legs > 0
            run.legs     += 1
            run.cls      := RN_CONT
            run.intToExt := run.intToExt or ext
        else
            run.dir      := nd
            run.legs     := 1
            run.cls      := RN_UNCONF
            run.intToExt := ext
            run.exhBar   := na
        run.lastBar := bar_index
        runFresh    := true
    else if run.dir != 0 and run.legs > 0 and run.cls != RN_REV
        // EXHAUSTION: the run reached the next pool on its own side and could
        // not break it — it was raided and rejected instead
        bool raidedSame = run.dir == 1 ? (bslInPlay and anyHiBar == bar_index) : (sslInPlay and anyLoBar == bar_index)
        if raidedSame
            run.cls    := RN_EXH
            run.exhBar := bar_index
    // REVERSAL: exhaustion, then an OPPOSING structural shift WITH displacement,
    // on a later bar. Everything short of that stays a continuation (§11).
    if run.cls == RN_EXH and not na(run.exhBar) and bar_index > run.exhBar
        bool shift = run.dir == 1 ? mssBear and (dispDnBar or recentDispDn) : mssBull and (dispUpBar or recentDispUp)
        if shift
            run.cls := RN_REV

// §11 made operational: a LONG taken while sell-side liquidity is still being
// RUN downward is a fade. It is permitted once the run has EXHAUSTED or actually
// REVERSED — never while it is still classified RN_CONT.
runFadeLong  = runGate != "Off" and run.dir == -1 and run.cls == RN_CONT
runFadeShort = runGate != "Off" and run.dir ==  1 and run.cls == RN_CONT

// ═══════════ V5.5-E5 · CONGESTION / COMPRESSION FILTER (§29)
// Price action only — §29 and §39 both rule out bolting an oscillator on for
// this. Four independent readings of "there is no narrative here":
//   TIGHT    the whole window fits inside a fraction of what its own ATR says it
//            should have covered — compression.
//   CHURN    most bars sit inside the previous bar's range.
//   NO NET   the window travelled almost nowhere relative to the distance its
//            candles actually covered — chop.
//   FLIP     structure broke in BOTH directions inside the window: no side is in
//            control, and every break was noise.
// Two of four = congested. congGate then downgrades one grade, or blocks.
congOvl   = math.min(high, nz(high[1], high)) - math.max(low, nz(low[1], low))
congOvlOk = rng > 0 and nz(rng[1], 0) > 0 and nz(congOvl, 0) >= 0.6 * math.max(rng, nz(rng[1], 0)) ? 1.0 : 0.0
congTight = ta.highest(high, congLen) - ta.lowest(low, congLen) <= 0.25 * congLen * atr   // V7.4 F-08 (was 0.45)
congChurn = ta.sma(congOvlOk, congLen) >= 0.6
congTrav  = ta.sma(rng, congLen) * congLen
congNoNet = congTrav > 0 and not na(close[congLen]) and math.abs(close - close[congLen]) / congTrav <= 0.15
congFlip  = math.sum((bosUp or chochUp) ? 1.0 : 0.0, congLen) >= 1 and math.sum((bosDn or chochDn) ? 1.0 : 0.0, congLen) >= 1
congN     = (congTight ? 1 : 0) + (congChurn ? 1 : 0) + (congNoNet ? 1 : 0) + (congFlip ? 1 : 0)
// V7.7 · F-06 · A MEASUREMENT IS NOT A POLICY. v7.6 folded the user's gate switch into
//   the reading itself (`congested = congGate != "Off" and congN >= 2`), and `regime`
//   then took its CHOP verdict from that. So turning the congestion FILTER off also
//   turned the chop MEASUREMENT off: the dashboard reported TREND or RANGE in obvious
//   chop, the §24 regime policy applied the wrong rule (a RANGE downgrade where CHOP
//   was the truth), and f_score's descriptor debit silently stopped being charged —
//   three unrelated consequences from one switch that only claimed to govern a gate.
congMeasured = congN >= 2                          // the reading — always true to itself
congested = congGate != "Off" and congMeasured     // the POLICY — gate + grade only
congHard  = congGate != "Downgrade"   // V7.5 · "A+ only" is hard too: no downgrade, the gate lets an A+ through

// ═══ V7.0 · §36 · MARKET REGIME ══════════════════════════════════
// Four readings, all from price action, all from series this file already
// computes: the congestion engine above, volatility against its own 50-bar
// mean, and where price sits in its own dealing range relative to the external
// structural direction. This is a CLASSIFICATION for the operator and for the
// score — it adds no new gate, because the gate that matters (congestion) is
// already wired and adding a second one on the same evidence would be exactly
// the over-filtering §46 warns against.
atrRel = atr / math.max(ta.sma(atr, 50), syminfo.mintick)
regime = congMeasured ? RG_CHOP : atrRel >= 1.4 ? RG_EXP :   // V7.7 F-06 · the reading, not the switch
     extDir != 0 and rangeOK and (extDir > 0 ? pdPos >= 0.5 : pdPos <= 0.5) ? RG_TREND : RG_RANGE

// ═══ V7.0 · §39 · AMD / POWER OF THREE / JUDAS ═══════════════════════
// ACCUMULATION is the Asian range building. MANIPULATION is a killzone opening
// by taking the liquidity that range (or the previous session, or the previous
// day) left behind — the Judas leg. DISTRIBUTION is what follows once structure
// has shifted and price is expanding away.
// These are CONTEXT. They never trigger anything: judasL / judasS only attach a
// descriptor to the liquidity event and feed the capped context bonus, exactly
// as §39 requires.
amdKz    = timeframe.isintraday and (inLon or inNY or inSB)
amdSes   = anyLoKind == PK_SES or anyHiKind == PK_SES or anyLoKind == PK_PD or anyHiKind == PK_PD
amdManip = amdKz and amdSes and ((sslInPlay and f_recent(anyLoBar, 8)) or (bslInPlay and f_recent(anyHiBar, 8)))
amdDist  = amdKz and not amdManip and (recentDispUp or recentDispDn) and (recentMssUp or recentMssDn)
amdTxt   = not timeframe.isintraday ? "—" : amdManip ? "MANIP" : amdDist ? "DISTRIB" : inAsia ? "ACCUM" : "—"
// V7.5 · §19 · a Judas leg is a MEANINGFUL manipulation with a confirmed
// reaction, not a raid inside a time window: raid grade ≥ B and the pool has
// reached PS_REACT (a shift / displacement AWAY from it on a later bar).
judasL   = amdManip and sslInPlay and f_recent(anyLoBar, 8) and anyLoRq >= RQ_B and f_poolState(anyLoPool) == PS_REACT
judasS   = amdManip and bslInPlay and f_recent(anyHiBar, 8) and anyHiRq >= RQ_B and f_poolState(anyHiPool) == PS_REACT

// ═══════════ V5.5-E · ATTACH THE DESCRIPTORS TO THE EVENT (§7 / §8)
// Every engine above, plus the trap engine and the session story, describes the
// SAME physical liquidity event whenever it fires on a side that has a live
// raid. They are written onto that ONE pool, so the dashboard, the debug panel
// and the entry reason can print "one event, four descriptors" — and so that
// f_score can pay for them ONCE, out of a capped allowance that is structurally
// unable to touch the liquidity block.
if confirmed
    if judasL
        f_evTag(anyLoPool, "JUDAS")
    if judasS
        f_evTag(anyHiPool, "JUDAS")
    if run.cls == RN_EXH and run.dir == -1
        f_evTag(anyLoPool, "RUN EXH")
    if run.cls == RN_EXH and run.dir == 1
        f_evTag(anyHiPool, "RUN EXH")


// ══════════════════════════════════ MODULE 12 · SETUP STATE MACHINE (V5-1/2/3/4)
// The ONLY path to a narrative entry. One live setup per direction.
//
//   0 NO SETUP → 1 HTF CONTEXT → 2 LIQUIDITY TARGET LOCKED → 3 THAT TARGET
//   RAIDED → 4 VALID POI AT THE RAID → 5 POST-RAID MSS → 6 DISPLACEMENT
//   → 7 FVG CREATED BY THAT DISPLACEMENT → 8 FVG RETEST = ENTRY → 9 SPENT
//
// Three things changed from v4 and they are the reason this module exists:
//
// 1 · CHRONOLOGY IS ENFORCED, NOT ASSUMED (V4-BUG-1). v4 ran these stages as a
//     straight run of independent `if s.st == ST_x` tests inside one call, and
//     every trigger could be true on one candle, so a single bar could walk
//     0 → 7. Here every stage stamps its bar and the next stage compares:
//
//        ctxBar < tgtBar < raidBar ≤ poiBar < mssBar < dispBar ≤ fvgBar < retestBar
//
//     The two ≤ pairs are the only physically simultaneous ones — the candle
//     that raids the pool can be the candle that taps the POI, and the
//     displacement candle IS the third candle of the gap it leaves. Everything
//     else needs a later bar, and a `moved` latch makes that structural rather
//     than incidental.
//
// 2 · EVERY EVENT IS OWNED (V4-BUG-3 / V4-BUG-2). The setup locks a target POOL
//     OBJECT at state 2 and state 3 fires only when THAT pool is raided, after
//     the lock. v4 asked f_findRaid for "the freshest raid on this side", so a
//     setup born at bar 103 could claim a raid from bar 100 on a pool it had
//     never been hunting.
//
// 3 · A DEAD SETUP CANNOT BE REBORN ON ITS OWN GRAVE (V4-BUG-4). Every
//     invalidation stamps resetBar, and state 0 → 1 refuses to arm on a bar
//     that already killed a setup.
//
// Nothing here emits. It reports [entry, blockReason] and MODULE 15 decides.
// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// dedupe memories — read by the machine, written by the TRADE ENGINE (V5-11)
var int lastPoolIdL = 0
var int lastPoolIdS = 0
var int lastPoiIdL  = 0
var int lastPoiIdS  = 0
var int lastMssBarL = na
var int lastMssBarS = na
// V5.5-E7 · the PRICE and bar of the last executed liquidity event, per side
var float lastPoolPxL  = na
var float lastPoolPxS  = na
var int   lastPoolBarL = na
var int   lastPoolBarS = na

type Setup
    int    st        = 0
    int    id        = 0
    int    bornBar   = na
    int    ctxBar    = na
    bool   reversal  = false
    int    tgtId     = 0     // stage 2 · the liquidity being hunted, by IDENTITY
    string tgtTag    = ""
    int    tgtBar    = na
    Pool   tgtPool   = na
    float  tgtW      = na    // V7.7 F-09 · the rank the locked target held, for the hysteresis
    int    liqId     = 0     // stage 3 · the raid of THAT pool, never another
    int    liqBar    = na
    float  liqExt    = na
    bool   liqStrong = false
    int    liqKind   = 0
    string liqTag    = ""
    int    liqQual   = 0
    Pool   pool      = na
    int    poiId     = 0     // stage 4 · the POI that sits AT that raid
    float  poiTop    = na
    float  poiBot    = na
    int    poiTier   = 3
    bool   poiFresh  = false
    bool   poiIsHtf  = false
    int    poiBar    = na
    Zone   poiZone   = na
    int    mssBar    = na    // stages 5 / 6 · the shift and its expansion
    bool   mssChoch  = false
    float  mssLvl    = na
    int    dispBar   = na
    float  dispBody  = na
    int    fvgBar    = na    // stage 7 · the gap that displacement left behind
    float  fvgTop    = na
    float  fvgBot    = na
    float  fvgH      = na
    int    retestBar = na    // stage 8 · execution
    int    retestQ   = 0
    // ── V7.0 · the impulse leg this setup created (§11), the inducement that
    //    paid for its raid (§38), when price FIRST entered the execution gap
    //    (§48 timing) and which PD-array class its POI is (§40).
    float  legLo     = na
    float  legHi     = na
    bool   induced   = false
    int    fvgTouch  = -1
    int    poiPa     = 0
    // ── V7.1 · the OTE BAND CLASS rather than a yes/no (§10), the number of
    //    DISCRETE touches of the execution gap (§48) and whether the setup's own
    //    displacement also left the dealing range (§2).
    int    oteQ      = 0
    int    fvgTouches = 0
    bool   inGap     = false
    bool   dispOutRng = false
    bool   sbWindow  = false
    int    resetBar  = na
    int    poiIn0    = 0     // V7.4 F-02 · HTF candles already inside the array at the tap
    int    poiHtfSeq = 0     // V7.7 F-04 · WHICH HTF array this is, not just where it was
    float  pbExt     = na    // V7.4 F-07 · deepest pullback since the FVG lock
    int    dispQ     = 0     // V7.5 · §12 · f_dispQ of the owned displacement candle

// Releases the pool binding, clears every owned field and stamps the bar, so
// the arming stage can refuse to re-arm on this candle (V4-BUG-4).
f_setupReset(Setup s) =>
    if not na(s.pool)
        if s.pool.bound == s.id
            s.pool.bound := 0
    // V7.7 · F-02 · release the POI the same way the pool is released, so the
    //   per-side cap can evict it again the moment this setup stops needing it.
    if not na(s.poiZone)
        if s.poiZone.bound == s.id
            s.poiZone.bound := 0
    s.st        := ST_NONE
    s.ctxBar    := na
    s.reversal  := false
    s.tgtId     := 0
    s.tgtTag    := ""
    s.tgtBar    := na
    s.tgtPool   := na
    s.tgtW      := na
    s.liqId     := 0
    s.liqBar    := na
    s.liqExt    := na
    s.liqStrong := false
    s.liqKind   := 0
    s.liqTag    := ""
    s.liqQual   := 0
    s.pool      := na
    s.poiId     := 0
    s.poiTop    := na
    s.poiBot    := na
    s.poiTier   := 3
    s.poiFresh  := false
    s.poiIsHtf  := false
    s.poiBar    := na
    s.poiZone   := na
    s.mssBar    := na
    s.mssChoch  := false
    s.mssLvl    := na
    s.dispBar   := na
    s.dispBody  := na
    s.fvgBar    := na
    s.fvgTop    := na
    s.fvgBot    := na
    s.fvgH      := na
    s.retestBar := na
    s.retestQ   := 0
    s.legLo     := na
    s.legHi     := na
    s.induced   := false
    s.fvgTouch  := -1
    s.poiPa     := 0
    s.oteQ       := 0        // V7.1
    s.fvgTouches := 0        // V7.1
    s.inGap      := false    // V7.1
    s.dispOutRng := false    // V7.1
    s.sbWindow  := false
    s.poiIn0    := 0         // V7.4
    s.poiHtfSeq := 0         // V7.7
    s.pbExt     := na        // V7.4
    s.dispQ     := 0         // V7.5
    s.resetBar  := bar_index
    true

// NARRATIVE INTEGRITY (§23). Re-derives the whole causal chain from the stored
// bars and ids at the moment of the entry, independently of the transitions
// that produced it. If this returns false the TRADE ENGINE emits nothing, no
// matter how high the soft score is.
f_narrOk(Setup s) =>
    bool ok = s.st == ST_ENTRY
    ok := ok and s.tgtId != 0 and s.liqId == s.tgtId          // raid hit the locked target
    ok := ok and nz(s.liqBar, -1) > nz(s.bornBar, 99999999)   // no retroactive raid
    ok := ok and nz(s.tgtBar, -1) >= nz(s.ctxBar, 99999999)
    ok := ok and nz(s.liqBar, -1) >  nz(s.tgtBar, 99999999)
    ok := ok and nz(s.poiBar, -1) >= nz(s.liqBar, 99999999)
    ok := ok and nz(s.mssBar, -1) >  nz(s.liqBar, 99999999)
    ok := ok and nz(s.mssBar, -1) >  nz(s.poiBar, 99999999)  // V5.2-B5: the machine can never make these equal
    ok := ok and (nz(s.dispBar, -1) > nz(s.mssBar, 99999999) or (mssIsDisp and nz(s.dispBar, -1) == nz(s.mssBar, -2)))   // V7.4 E2-B
    ok := ok and nz(s.fvgBar, -1) >= nz(s.dispBar, 99999999)
    ok := ok and nz(s.fvgBar, -1) - nz(s.dispBar, -99999999) <= FVG_OWN_MAX
    ok := ok and nz(s.retestBar, -1) > nz(s.fvgBar, 99999999)
    ok

// ═══ V7.0 · §10 / §11 · TRUE OTE ══════════════════════════════════
// v6.4 asked one global question — "is close inside the 62-79 % band of the
// CHART'S dealing range?" — and handed the same answer to every setup on the
// bar. A retracement is a property of ONE impulse leg, and the leg that
// matters is the one this setup created: its own raid extreme (where the move
// originated) to the extreme its own displacement reached.
//   `deep` is the retest candle's extreme — how far price actually pulled back.
//   `px`   is the entry price — used only to prove the leg origin still holds.
// Reaching 62 % is the requirement; running past 79 % is not a disqualifier by
// itself (it is a deeper discount), but breaking the leg origin is, because at
// that point there is no leg left to retrace into.
// V7.1 · GRADED, NOT BOOLEAN. A 63 % retracement and a 92 % one paid the same
// four points and were equally A+ eligible, which is exactly backwards: the
// deeper the pullback runs past 79 %, the less leg is left to trade. The band is
// returned as a CLASS — 1 = 62-70 % (prime, full points, A+ eligible) · 2 =
// 70-79 % (deep; still tradable and still scored, but capped at grade A) · 3 =
// beyond 79 % (one token point, never A+) · 0 = never reached 62 %, or the leg
// origin has been broken and there is no leg left to retrace into.
f_inOte(bool isL, float lo, float hi, float deep, float px) =>
    float w = nz(hi - lo, 0)
    int   q = 0
    if w > 0 and not na(deep) and not na(px) and (isL ? px > lo : px < hi)
        float r = isL ? (hi - deep) / w : (deep - lo) / w
        q := r > OTE_2 ? 3 : r > OTE_M ? 2 : r >= OTE_1 ? 1 : 0
    q

// P1-2 · THE HTF POI TIER IS DYNAMIC NOW. v7.2 stamped tier 1 at the tap and the
// live re-grade skipped `poiIsHtf` by design, so a FEM setup kept its A+ eligibility
// after the HTF array had been mitigated and after the HTF itself had flipped
// against it. Same monotone-worse ladder f_regrade applies to chart zones, off the
// two things the HTF feed already reports: how many HTF candles have traded into the
// array (hPoiBIn / hPoiSIn, the mitigation reading) and whether the HTF bias still
// agrees. Fresh and aligned = 1; one candle inside = 2; more = 3; an opposed HTF
// costs one further tier. It can only ever get WORSE than tier 1.
// V7.4 F-02 · the HTF candle that CONTAINS the setup's own tap counts once it
// closes; `in0` is the count at the tap and exactly one further candle is
// forgiven, so a fresh, aligned array stays tier 1 until a SECOND HTF candle
// trades into it or the HTF opposes.
f_htfTier(bool isL, int in0) =>
    int(math.min(1 + math.max(nz(isL ? hPoiBIn : hPoiSIn, 0) - in0 - 1, 0) + ((isL ? htfDir >= 0 : htfDir <= 0) ? 0 : 1), 3))

f_setupAdvance(Setup s, int dir) =>
    bool   entry = false
    string blk   = ""
    bool   isL   = dir == 1
    // at most ONE transition per bar. The two exceptions below are the pairs
    // that are simultaneous by construction, and they are named explicitly so
    // no future edit can widen them by accident.
    bool   moved = false
    // V5.6-L12 · TOKEN BUDGET. f_setupReset is a ~37-write body and v5.6 called
    // it at NINETEEN sites inside this function, which is itself instantiated
    // twice — Pine inlines every call, so that was ~7 000 compiled tokens of one
    // reset. Every invalidation now raises `kill`; the reset runs ONCE, at the
    // end of the bar. Behaviour is identical: each stage guard carries
    // `not kill`, so nothing can advance after a kill on the same bar, exactly
    // as nothing could advance from ST_NONE before. The terminal-state clear
    // below stays a direct reset because the 0 → 1 block must see it this bar.
    bool   kill  = false
    if confirmed
        // ── 9 · terminal states clear on a LATER bar, so the engine always had
        //      one full bar in which to consume an ENTRY
        if s.st == ST_ENTRY or s.st == ST_DEAD
            if s.retestBar != bar_index
                f_setupReset(s)
        // ── invalidation, checked before any advance
        if s.st > ST_NONE and s.st < ST_ENTRY
            // V5.3-C1: refresh the owned POI tier from the LIVE zone before
            // judging anything. f_regrade decays tiers every bar; a grade or a
            // gate reading the stage-4 snapshot would trade the tier the zone
            // HAD, not the tier it HAS. V7.3 · HTF POIs are re-graded here too.
            // P1-2 · HTF POIs decay too now. f_zoneTier is already na-safe (na -> 3),
            //   so the zone branch needs no guard of its own.
            if s.st >= ST_POI
                s.poiTier := s.poiIsHtf ? f_htfTier(isL, s.poiIn0) : f_zoneTier(s.poiZone)
            // §11 · the leg extends while the move is still being made, and is
            //       frozen the moment displacement completes it. Extending it
            //       through the retracement would move the OTE band under the
            //       entry it is supposed to be judging.
            if s.st >= ST_RAID and s.st <= ST_DISP
                if isL
                    s.legHi := math.max(nz(s.legHi, high), high)
                else
                    s.legLo := math.min(nz(s.legLo, low), low)
            if bar_index - s.bornBar > setupLife
                blk := "setup expired"
                kill := true
            else if isL ? eChochDn : eChochUp
                blk := "opposite external CHoCH"
                kill := true
            else if s.st >= ST_RAID and f_poolSpent(s.pool)
                blk := "target pool already produced an entry"
                kill := true
            else if s.st >= ST_RAID and not na(s.liqExt) and (isL ? close < s.liqExt : close > s.liqExt)
                blk := "raid failed (closed beyond the wick)"
                kill := true
            else if s.st >= ST_RAID and nz(f_poolBrkBar(s.pool), -1) > nz(s.liqBar, -1)
                // V5.2-B4: a close back THROUGH the swept level is a failed
                // sweep even while it has not yet crossed the raid wick.
                blk := "raid level reclaimed (close back through the pool)"
                kill := true
            else if s.st >= ST_POI and s.poiIsHtf and bar_index - s.poiBar > femWait
                blk := "FEM window expired"
                kill := true
            // V7.7 · F-04 · THE HTF ARRAY THIS SETUP BOUND MUST STILL BE THE ONE THE
            //   FEED IS SERVING. The mitigation counters f_htfTier reads reset whenever
            //   the feed stamps a new gap or retires the old one, so without this the
            //   tier ratchet silently ran BACKWARDS: a decayed T2 / T3 HTF POI returned
            //   to T1 (and to A+ eligibility) the moment the HTF printed its next gap.
            //   This is the HTF twin of the zone-side `f_zoneDead` check two lines down.
            else if s.st >= ST_POI and s.poiIsHtf and s.poiHtfSeq != nz(isL ? hPoiBSeq : hPoiSSeq, 0)
                blk := "HTF POI replaced or retired — the array this setup bound is gone"
                kill := true
            else if s.st >= ST_POI and not na(s.poiBot) and (isL ? close < s.poiBot - NEAR_LVL_ATR * atr : close > s.poiTop + NEAR_LVL_ATR * atr)
                blk := "POI invalidated"
                kill := true
            else if s.st >= ST_POI and not s.poiIsHtf and f_zoneDead(s.poiZone)
                blk := "POI spent or re-graded below the minimum tier"
                kill := true
            // V7.1 · §2 · AN EXPANSION THAT IS GIVEN BACK WAS NOT A DISPLACEMENT.
            //   While the setup is still waiting for its gap, a close back through
            //   the level the MSS broke says the delivery failed. v7.0 let that
            //   setup carry on and hand its FVG to the entry stage anyway.
            else if s.st == ST_DISP and not na(s.mssLvl) and (isL ? close < s.mssLvl : close > s.mssLvl)
                blk := "displacement reversed back through the MSS level"
                kill := true
        // ── 0 → 1 · HTF CONTEXT (and the CONTINUATION / REVERSAL verdict)
        if s.st == ST_NONE and not moved and not kill
            if s.resetBar == bar_index
                blk := blk == "" ? "setup invalidated this bar — no same-bar re-arm" : blk
            else if (isL ? htfIsRevLong : htfIsRevShort) and not allowRev
                // V5.1-A3c: allowRev = OFF now means what it says. v5 only wired
                // allowRev into htfRevLong, which needHtfAlign = false bypassed.
                blk := "counter-HTF setup and REVERSAL setups are disabled"
            else if isL ? htfCtxLong : htfCtxShort
                s.st       := ST_CTX
                s.id       := bar_index * 2 + (isL ? 1 : 2)
                s.bornBar  := bar_index
                s.ctxBar   := bar_index
                s.reversal := isL ? htfIsRevLong : htfIsRevShort
                moved      := true
            else
                blk := "HTF context not aligned"
        // ── 1 → 2 · LOCK THE LIQUIDITY TARGET (identity, not just a price)
        if s.st == ST_CTX and not moved and not kill
            Pool tp = isL ? tgtSellPool : tgtBuyPool
            if na(tp)
                blk := "no liquidity target"
            else if strictSeq and bar_index <= s.ctxBar
                blk := "context armed this bar — target locks next bar"
            else
                s.st      := ST_TGT
                s.tgtId   := tp.id
                s.tgtTag  := tp.tag
                s.tgtPool := tp
                s.tgtW    := isL ? tgtSellW : tgtBuyW   // V7.7 F-09
                s.tgtBar  := bar_index
                moved     := true
        // ── 2 → 3 · THAT POOL IS RAIDED, after the lock (V4-BUG-2 / V4-BUG-3)
        if s.st == ST_TGT and not moved and not kill
            Pool tp2 = s.tgtPool
            // V7.4 E2-A · any qualifying pool raided AFTER the lock may become the
            //   bound target. The ranked pool keeps priority when it was itself raided.
            if tgtAny
                Pool ap = f_anyRaid(dir, nz(s.tgtBar, -1), tgtMinPri)
                if not na(ap)
                    bool sw = na(tp2)
                    if not sw
                        sw := tp2.state != PS_RAID and tp2.state != PS_REACT
                    if sw
                        tp2       := ap
                        s.tgtId   := ap.id
                        s.tgtTag  := ap.tag
                        s.tgtPool := ap
            if na(tp2)
                blk := "target lost"
                kill := true
            else if tp2.state == PS_DONE
                blk := "target pool broken or expired"
                kill := true
            else if tp2.state == PS_RAID or tp2.state == PS_REACT
                bool chrono = nz(tp2.raidBar, -1) > nz(s.tgtBar, 99999999) or (not strictSeq and nz(tp2.raidBar, -1) >= nz(s.tgtBar, 99999999))
                if not chrono
                    blk := "raid pre-dates the target lock — retroactive claim refused"
                    kill := true
                else if tp2.bound != 0 and tp2.bound != s.id
                    blk := "raid already bound to another setup"
                    kill := true
                else if tp2.spent
                    blk := "pool already produced an entry"
                    kill := true
                else if not f_recent(tp2.raidBar, sweepLook)
                    blk := "raid is stale"
                    kill := true
                else
                    s.st        := ST_RAID
                    s.liqId     := tp2.id
                    s.liqBar    := tp2.raidBar
                    s.liqExt    := tp2.raidExt
                    s.liqStrong := tp2.raidStrong
                    s.liqKind   := tp2.kind
                    s.liqTag    := tp2.tag
                    s.liqQual   := tp2.rq
                    s.pool      := tp2
                    tp2.bound   := s.id
                    // §11 · the impulse leg opens AT the raid extreme — that is
                    //       where the move this setup is trading began.
                    s.legLo     := isL ? tp2.raidExt : low
                    s.legHi     := isL ? high : tp2.raidExt
                    // §38 · was a minor pool taken first, to pay for this raid?
                    int indB = isL ? indLoBar : indHiBar
                    int indI = isL ? indLoId  : indHiId
                    s.induced   := useInduce and indB >= 0 and indI != tp2.id and
                         indB < nz(tp2.raidBar, 0) and nz(tp2.raidBar, 0) - indB <= induceLook
                    moved       := true
            else
                // still hunting. The best-ranked live pool is what price is actually
                // working toward, so the target may be RE-SELECTED — but the
                // clock restarts with it, so the raid can never pre-date it.
                // V7.7 · F-09 · AND THEREFORE IT MAY NOT RESTART ON NOISE. The ranking
                //   is level quality minus tgtDistW per ATR of distance, so two pools of
                //   comparable standing trade places as price drifts between them. Every
                //   swap reset tgtBar, and stage 2 → 3 demands `raidBar > tgtBar`, so a
                //   setup parked between two such pools could never bind a raid at all —
                //   it simply aged out at setupLife. A challenger must now beat the
                //   LOCKED pool's live rank by TGT_HYST; a pool that has died, been spent
                //   or drifted out of range is replaced unconditionally, as before.
                Pool  tp3 = isL ? tgtSellPool : tgtBuyPool
                float w3  = isL ? tgtSellW : tgtBuyW
                if not na(tp3)
                    if tp3.id == s.tgtId
                        s.tgtW := w3            // same target, refreshed rank — no clock reset
                    else
                        bool lost = na(s.tgtPool)
                        if not lost
                            lost := s.tgtPool.state >= PS_DONE or s.tgtPool.spent
                        if lost or na(s.tgtW) or w3 > s.tgtW + TGT_HYST
                            s.tgtId   := tp3.id
                            s.tgtTag  := tp3.tag
                            s.tgtPool := tp3
                            s.tgtW    := w3
                            s.tgtBar  := bar_index
                blk := "target not yet raided"
        // ── 3 → 4 · A VALID POI AT THE RAID.  raid candle = POI candle is legal
        if s.st == ST_RAID and not kill and (not moved or s.liqBar == bar_index)
            if bar_index - s.liqBar > mssWin
                blk := "raid went stale before a POI"
                kill := true
            else
                // V7.1 · §7 · BINDING IS CONTAINMENT NOW. v7.0 accepted any array whose
                //   NEAREST EDGE fell within poiBindAtr (default 2.0) ATR of the raid
                //   extreme. Two ATR from the wick is not "the POI at the liquidity",
                //   it is a different trade with a borrowed reason. With poiStrictBind
                //   on, the array has to hold the wick that took the pool.
                float pTol = poiBindAtr * atr
                float hTop = isL ? hPoiBTop : hPoiSTop
                float hBot = isL ? hPoiBBot : hPoiSBot
                bool  hTap = useFEM and not na(hTop) and (isL ? low <= hTop and close > hBot : high >= hBot and close < hTop) and s.liqExt <= hTop + pTol and s.liqExt >= hBot - pTol
                if hTap
                    s.st       := ST_POI
                    s.poiTop   := hTop
                    s.poiBot   := hBot
                    s.poiIn0   := isL ? nz(hPoiBIn, 0) : nz(hPoiSIn, 0)   // V7.4 F-02
                    s.poiHtfSeq := isL ? nz(hPoiBSeq, 0) : nz(hPoiSSeq, 0)   // V7.7 F-04
                    s.poiTier  := f_htfTier(isL, s.poiIn0)
                    s.poiFresh := s.poiIn0 == 0
                    s.poiIsHtf := true
                    s.poiId    := 0
                    s.poiZone  := na
                    s.poiBar   := bar_index
                    moved      := true
                else
                    // chart-TF POI: the best eligible zone that CONTAINS the raid
                    float zTopB  = na
                    float zBotB  = na
                    int   zTierB = 9
                    int   zwB    = 999
                    int   zPaB   = 0
                    int   zIdB   = 0
                    bool  zFrB   = false
                    Zone  zRef   = na
                    if array.size(zones) > 0
                        for i = 0 to array.size(zones) - 1
                            Zone z = array.get(zones, i)
                            bool sideOk = isL ? not z.isSupply : z.isSupply
                            bool dedupe = dedupePoi and z.id == (isL ? lastPoiIdL : lastPoiIdS)
                            // V7.1 · §40 · AGE. A zone that has been on the chart for
                            //   hundreds of bars has been mitigated and re-priced many
                            //   times over, whatever its live tier still reads.
                            bool live   = not z.signaled and not dedupe and (not fvgFillDead or z.state < 3) and z.tier <= minTier and bar_index - z.bornBar <= poiMaxAge and z.bornBar <= s.liqBar   // V7.4 F-05
                            // §40 · Two live arrays can both contain the raid; ICT ranks
                            //   an order block above a raw gap and a mitigation block
                            //   below both, so the tie is broken by what the array IS,
                            //   not by which was found first.
                            // V7.1 · tier first · then the PD-array ladder (order block >
                            //   breaker > IFVG > BPR > RAW FVG > mitigation) · then how
                            //   little of it has been eaten. v7.0 ranked the first two
                            //   and broke the remaining ties on array order.
                            int zw = z.tier * 100 + f_paRank(z.pa) * 10 + z.state
                            if sideOk and live and zw < zwB
                                float zt = z.top    // V5.4-D1
                                float zb = z.bot
                                if s.liqExt <= zt + pTol and s.liqExt >= zb - pTol
                                    zTopB  := zt
                                    zBotB  := zb
                                    zwB    := zw
                                    zTierB := z.tier
                                    zPaB   := z.pa
                                    zIdB   := z.id
                                    zFrB   := z.state <= 1
                                    zRef   := z
                    if not na(zTopB)
                        s.st       := ST_POI
                        s.poiTop   := zTopB
                        s.poiBot   := zBotB
                        s.poiTier  := zTierB
                        s.poiFresh := zFrB
                        s.poiIsHtf := false
                        s.poiId    := zIdB
                        s.poiPa    := zPaB
                        s.poiZone  := zRef
                        zRef.bound := s.id      // V7.7 · F-02 · the cap may not evict it now
                        s.poiBar   := bar_index
                        moved      := true
                    else
                        blk := "no valid POI at the raid"
        // ── 4 → 5 · POST-RAID MSS (V4-BUG-6). Strictly after the raid, breaking
        //      a level that formed around it, and far enough from the raid
        //      extreme that it is a shift rather than the sweep candle again.
        if s.st == ST_POI and not moved and not kill
            if bar_index - s.poiBar > mssWin
                blk := "MSS window expired"
                kill := true
            else if bar_index <= s.liqBar
                blk := "awaiting a POST-RAID shift"
            else
                bool  ch   = isL ? chochUp : chochDn
                // §5 · cisdMssUp/Dn already carry the displacement requirement, so
                //      a bare change in the state of delivery can no longer be
                //      promoted to a market-structure shift.
                bool  ci   = isL ? cisdMssUp : cisdMssDn
                float lvl  = ch ? (isL ? sInt.evLvlUp : sInt.evLvlDn) : (isL ? bearRunOpen : bullRunOpen)
                int   lvlB = ch ? (isL ? sInt.evBarUp : sInt.evBarDn) : (isL ? bearRunBar : bullRunBar)
                bool  rel  = not na(lvl) and nz(lvlB, -99999999) >= s.liqBar - mssWin
                bool  sep  = not na(lvl) and math.abs(lvl - s.liqExt) >= MSS_MIN_SEP_ATR * atr
                if (ch or (ci and not mssNeedChoch)) and rel and sep
                    s.st       := ST_MSS
                    s.mssBar   := bar_index
                    s.mssChoch := ch
                    s.mssLvl   := lvl
                    moved      := true
                    // V7.4 E2-B · the MSS candle may itself be the displacement when it
                    //   passes the four stage-6 tests (`past` holds by construction).
                    if mssIsDisp and (isL ? dispUpBar : dispDnBar) and body >= dispBodyAtr * atr and (isL ? close > s.poiTop + POI_LEAVE_ATR * atr : close < s.poiBot - POI_LEAVE_ATR * atr) and (isL ? close - low : high - close) / math.max(high - low, syminfo.mintick) >= DISP_HOLD_FRAC
                        s.st         := ST_DISP
                        s.dispBar    := bar_index
                        s.dispBody   := body / atr
                        s.dispQ      := isL ? dqUp : dqDn
                        s.dispOutRng := rangeOK and (isL ? close > rngHi : close < rngLo)
                else if ch or ci
                    blk := not rel ? "MSS broke an unrelated (pre-raid) level" : "MSS level is the raid itself"
                else
                    blk := "MSS / CHoCH missing"
        // ── 5 → 6 · DISPLACEMENT (V4-BUG-7). Strictly after the MSS, closing
        //      beyond the level the MSS broke and out of the POI it came from.
        if s.st == ST_MSS and not moved and not kill
            if bar_index - s.mssBar > dispWin
                blk := "displacement window expired"
                kill := true
            else
                // V7.1 · §2 · FOUR REQUIREMENTS, NOT TWO. A displacement has to be a
                //   BODY (dispBodyAtr, its own input on top of the chart-wide marker),
                //   it has to close BEYOND the level the MSS broke, it has to close
                //   OUTSIDE the POI it came from, and it has to HOLD what it took into
                //   the close. A candle that expands and then hands most of it back
                //   inside the same bar is a liquidity grab, not delivery, and v7.0
                //   accepted it as stage 6 on the strength of its body alone.
                float dRng = math.max(high - low, syminfo.mintick)
                bool  dsp  = (isL ? dispUpBar : dispDnBar) and body >= dispBodyAtr * atr
                bool  past = not na(s.mssLvl) and (isL ? close > s.mssLvl : close < s.mssLvl)
                bool  away = isL ? close > s.poiTop + POI_LEAVE_ATR * atr : close < s.poiBot - POI_LEAVE_ATR * atr
                bool  hold = (isL ? close - low : high - close) / dRng >= DISP_HOLD_FRAC
                if dsp and past and away and hold
                    s.st         := ST_DISP
                    s.dispBar    := bar_index
                    s.dispBody   := body / atr
                    s.dispQ      := isL ? dqUp : dqDn     // V7.5 · §12
                    // §10 · expansion that ALSO leaves the dealing range is the
                    //   strongest reading of the same candle: the move is no longer a
                    //   retracement inside a range, it is a new leg. Preferred in the
                    //   score, never required — causality first, confluence after.
                    s.dispOutRng := rangeOK and (isL ? close > rngHi : close < rngLo)
                    moved        := true
                else if dsp and past and away
                    blk := "weak displacement — closed back inside its own range"
                else if dsp
                    blk := not past ? "expansion did not clear the MSS level" : "expansion did not leave the POI"
                else
                    blk := "displacement missing"
        // ── 6 → 7 · THE FVG THAT DISPLACEMENT ITSELF CREATED (V4-BUG-8).
        //      The displacement candle must be one of the three candles that
        //      form the gap, which is what "the displacement's FVG" means.
        if s.st == ST_DISP and not kill and (not moved or s.dispBar == bar_index)
            if bar_index - s.dispBar > FVG_OWN_MAX
                // Plenty of real expansion candles leave no gap. That does not
                // invalidate the shift that produced them, so the setup falls
                // back to state 5 and waits for the next expansion — still
                // strictly after the same MSS — instead of being destroyed.
                if bar_index - s.mssBar <= dispWin
                    s.st         := ST_MSS
                    s.dispBar    := na
                    s.dispBody   := na
                    s.dispOutRng := false   // V7.1 · clears with the displacement it described
                    blk := "that expansion left no FVG — awaiting another"
                else
                    blk := "no FVG created by the displacement leg"
                    kill := true
            // P1-3 · POST-DISPLACEMENT INTEGRITY. The gap may not be locked on the
            //   displacement bar itself. One further confirmed bar has to print, and
            //   it has to still hold what the expansion took: outside the POI the
            //   move left, and — through the ST_DISP reversal kill above — beyond the
            //   level the MSS broke. A candle that expands and is handed straight
            //   back can no longer advance the setup to its execution array.
            //   FVG_OWN_MAX still allows two bars, and an expansion that leaves no
            //   gap still falls back to ST_MSS to wait for the next one.
            else if bar_index == s.dispBar
                blk := "awaiting post-displacement confirmation"
            else if not (isL ? close > s.poiTop : close < s.poiBot)
                blk := "expansion given back into the POI"
            else if (isL ? bullFVG : bearFVG) or (bar_index == s.dispBar + 1 and (isL ? bullFVG[1] : bearFVG[1]))
                // V7.4 F-06 · the gap whose THIRD candle was the displacement is read one
                //   bar late, because P1-3 forbids locking on the displacement bar.
                bool  g0 = isL ? bullFVG : bearFVG
                float ft = g0 ? (isL ? low : low[2]) : (isL ? low[1] : low[3])
                float fb = g0 ? (isL ? high[2] : high) : (isL ? high[3] : high[1])
                if ft - fb >= fvgMinAtr * atr and (isL ? close > fb : close < ft)
                    s.st     := ST_FVG
                    s.fvgTop := ft
                    s.fvgBot := fb
                    s.fvgH   := ft - fb
                    s.fvgBar := bar_index
                    moved    := true
                else
                    blk := "FVG below the minimum height"
            else
                blk := "awaiting the FVG"
        // ── 7 → 8 · FVG RETEST = ENTRY (§10). A touch is not an entry: the gap
        //      must be penetrated to retestDepth AND the candle must close back
        //      through its 50 % with a rejection.
        if s.st == ST_FVG and not moved and not kill
            float h   = math.max(nz(s.fvgH, syminfo.mintick), syminfo.mintick)
            float mid = isL ? s.fvgTop - 0.5 * h : s.fvgBot + 0.5 * h
            float nd  = isL ? s.fvgTop - retestDepth * h : s.fvgBot + retestDepth * h
            float pen = isL ? (s.fvgTop - low) / h : (high - s.fvgBot) / h
            if isL ? close < s.fvgBot : close > s.fvgTop
                blk := "FVG invalidated (traded fully through)"
                kill := true
            else if bar_index - s.fvgBar > retestWin
                blk := "retest window expired"
                kill := true
            else if bar_index <= s.fvgBar
                blk := "awaiting the FVG retest"
            else
                // §48 · the clock starts when price FIRST enters the gap, not when
                //       the gap was made. Everything after that is the market
                //       deciding, and a rejection that takes many candles to
                //       arrive is a LATE entry into a mitigated array.
                // V7.1 · §48 · DISCRETE TOUCHES, not just the first one. Every fresh
                //   entry into the gap after having been fully outside it is one more
                //   mitigation of that array. lateBars still runs off the FIRST touch;
                //   the COUNT is what the A+ rung reads (apMaxTouch).
                bool inG = isL ? low <= s.fvgTop : high >= s.fvgBot
                if inG and not s.inGap
                    s.fvgTouches := s.fvgTouches + 1
                    if s.fvgTouch == -1
                        s.fvgTouch := bar_index
                s.inGap := inG
                bool deep = isL ? low <= nd : high >= nd
                bool back = isL ? close >= mid and close > s.fvgBot : close <= mid and close < s.fvgTop
                bool rej  = isL ? zoneBullConf or strongUp : zoneBearConf or strongDn
                bool late = s.fvgTouch >= 0 and bar_index - s.fvgTouch > lateBars
                // §11 · the OTE verdict is recorded on the setup at the moment of
                //       execution, from ITS leg, and is read by the score and by
                //       the A+ precondition. oteLegMode OFF falls back to the
                //       chart dealing range, which is what v6.4 always used.
                // V7.1 · it is a BAND CLASS, not a flag — 0 none · 1 prime 62-70 % ·
                //   2 deep 70-79 % (grade A max) · 3 beyond 79 % (never A+).
                s.pbExt := isL ? math.min(nz(s.pbExt, low), low) : math.max(nz(s.pbExt, high), high)   // V7.4 F-07
                s.oteQ := oteLegMode ? f_inOte(isL, s.legLo, s.legHi, s.pbExt, close)
                     : ((isL ? inOTEbuy : inOTEsell) ? 1 : 0)
                if late
                    blk := "LATE — " + str.tostring(bar_index - s.fvgTouch) + " bars since the first FVG touch"
                    kill := true
                else if deep and back and rej
                    s.st        := ST_ENTRY
                    s.retestBar := bar_index
                    s.retestQ   := pen >= 0.8 ? 3 : pen >= 0.5 ? 2 : 1
                    s.sbWindow  := sbActive
                    entry       := true
                    moved       := true
                else if not deep
                    blk := "awaiting the FVG retest"
                else if not back
                    blk := "no close back through the FVG 50 %"
                else
                    blk := "retest without a rejection candle"
        // ── the ONE reset site (V5.6-L12). Same bar, same fields, same
        //      resetBar stamp — only the number of inlined copies changed.
        if kill
            f_setupReset(s)
    [entry, blk]

var Setup upS = Setup.new()
var Setup dnS = Setup.new()
[machLong,  blkLong]  = f_setupAdvance(upS, 1)
[machShort, blkShort] = f_setupAdvance(dnS, -1)
narrLong  = f_narrOk(upS)
narrShort = f_narrOk(dnS)

// ═══════════════════════════════════ MODULE 13 · ALIGNMENT SCORE (SOFT ONLY)
// V4-BUG-5 — v4's f_score read GLOBAL "did this happen in the last N bars"
// flags: recentMssUp, recentDispUp, cisdUpBar, raidExtLo, raidEqLo, sslStrong.
// None of them belonged to the setup being scored, so a dead setup's CHoCH, a
// sweep on an unrelated pool and a displacement from the other side of the
// chart all paid into one number. In v5 every ingredient is passed in BY the
// caller from the fields of the setup (or of the specific producer) being
// scored, and nothing is read from a global event memory.
//
//   CONTEXT   25 = HTF agreement 10 · external structure 6 · premium/discount 5
//                  · session narrative 4
//   LIQUIDITY 25 = raid grade 12 · pool class 8 · pool quality 5
//   POI       20 = HTF POI 6 · freshness 5 · live tier 5 · OTE 4
//   TRIGGER   20 = MSS grade 8 · displacement QUALITY 6 · MSS ≠ displacement
//                  bar 3 · owned gap ≥ FVG_GOOD_ATR 3            (V7.5)
//   EXECUTION 10 = retest quality 6 · rejection candle 4
//
// The score RANKS things that have already passed every hard gate. It is not a
// win probability and it can never create a grade on its own (V5-7).
// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
sesPts = not timeframe.isintraday ? 4 : inSB or inNY or inLon ? 4 : inAsia ? 2 : 0

// V5-12 · SESSION NARRATIVE. A session is not just a permitted clock window —
// London taking Asia's low and New York taking London's low are the two most
// common liquidity stories on an intraday chart, and they are recognised here
// as CONTEXT, not as a filter. V7.5 · read from the TRADED event's tag / class
// (MODULE 15), never from the freshest raid on the side.
f_sesStory(string tag, int kind) =>
    timeframe.isintraday and tag != "" and ((inLon and str.contains(tag, "Asia")) or ((inNY or inSbAM or inSbPM) and str.contains(tag, "Lon")) or (inSB and kind == PK_SES) or ((inLon or inNY) and kind == PK_PD))

f_score(int dir, int tier, bool isHtfPoi, bool fresh, int retQ, bool rejOk, int rq, int rkind, int touches, bool rev, bool mssCh, bool mssOk, int dispQ, int mssB, int dispB, float fvgAtr, bool story, int descN, bool cong, bool induced, int oteQ, bool atHtfArr, bool dispOut) =>
    bool isL = dir == 1
    // ── CONTEXT 25
    int ctx = (isL ? htfDir > 0 : htfDir < 0) ? 10 : rev ? 4 : 0
    ctx += (isL ? extDir > 0 : extDir < 0) ? 6 : 0
    ctx += rangeOK and (isL ? pdPos < 0.5 : pdPos >= 0.5) ? 5 : 0
    ctx += story ? 4 : sesPts >= 4 ? 3 : sesPts
    // ── LIQUIDITY 25 — the grade of THIS setup's raid on THIS setup's pool
    int liq = rq == RQ_A ? 12 : rq == RQ_B ? 8 : rq == RQ_C ? 4 : 0
    liq += rkind == PK_HTF or rkind == PK_PW ? 8 : rkind == PK_EXT or rkind == PK_PD or rkind == PK_SES ? 8 : rkind == PK_EQ ? 6 : rkind == PK_TL ? 5 : rkind == PK_SWING ? 3 : 0
    // §29 · NO DOUBLE COUNTING. Inducement is a statement about the QUALITY of
    //     this raid, so it is paid out of the same five points prior respect
    //     was paid from — the LIQUIDITY block stays capped at 25 and a setup
    //     cannot be paid twice for one liquidity event.
    liq += (touches >= 2 ? 3 : touches == 1 ? 2 : 0) + (induced ? 2 : 0)
    // ── POI 20
    // §26 · an LTF entry sitting at an HTF PD array is worth something even when
    //     the HTF array is not itself the POI — but not as much, and out of the
    //     same six points, never in addition to them.
    int poi = isHtfPoi ? 6 : atHtfArr ? 3 : 0
    poi += fresh ? 5 : 0
    poi += tier == 1 ? 5 : tier == 2 ? 3 : 0
    // §11 · the setup's OWN OTE, not the chart's
    // V7.1 · the prime 62-70 % band is worth the full four; a 70-79 % deep entry
    //   is worth two; beyond 79 % is a token one. Same four-point allowance.
    poi += oteQ == 1 ? 4 : oteQ == 2 ? 2 : oteQ == 3 ? 1 : 0
    // ── TRIGGER 20 — independent evidence only
    int trg = mssOk ? (mssCh ? 8 : 5) : 0
    // V7.5 · §12 · displacement QUALITY (f_dispQ: body · 2× body · hold · strong
    //   hold · wick asymmetry · relative expansion) instead of body size alone; a
    //   displacement that also left the dealing range still reads as the strongest
    //   form. Same six points, so the TRIGGER block stays capped at 20.
    trg += dispQ >= 5 or (dispOut and dispQ >= 3) ? 6 : dispQ >= 3 ? 4 : dispQ >= 1 ? 2 : 0
    trg += mssOk and nz(dispB, -1) != nz(mssB, -2) ? 3 : 0
    // V7.5 · F-10 · the GLOBAL "independent CISD" term is gone (V4-BUG-5 class).
    //   Its three points now pay for a gap of institutional size in the OWNED leg.
    trg += fvgAtr >= FVG_GOOD_ATR ? 3 : 0
    // ── EXECUTION 10
    int exe = retQ >= 3 ? 6 : retQ == 2 ? 4 : retQ == 1 ? 2 : 0
    exe += rejOk ? 4 : 0
    // ── EVENT DESCRIPTORS ≤ 6 (V5.5-E · §8). This is the ONLY place JUDAS, run
    //    exhaustion and the other event descriptors are worth anything in the
    //    score, they are worth it ONCE no matter how many of them fired, and the
    //    allowance is structurally unable to reach the LIQUIDITY block — which is
    //    still paid exactly once, from this event's own raid grade, pool class and
    //    touch history. Congestion is a debit against the same allowance, so a
    //    descriptor pile-up inside chop nets to nothing. The total is clamped at
    //    100 so the score stays a normalised 0-100 reading (§27).
    int ev = descN >= 3 ? 6 : descN == 2 ? 4 : descN == 1 ? 2 : 0
    ev := math.max(cong ? ev - 4 : ev, 0)
    // V7.9 · RECLAIM R3 · the five block sub-totals were returned and never read.
    //   Every point they carry is already inside the total; the descriptor allowance
    //   `ev` is kept because the debug panel prints it. Same score, same caps.
    [ev, math.min(ctx + liq + poi + trg + exe + ev, 100)]

// ═══════════════════════════════ MODULE 14 · BASE GATES + DEDUPE
// These filters SILENCE a signal. They never advance or consume state.
// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
var int lastLongSigBar  = na
var int lastShortSigBar = na
longCdOK  = masterCd == 0 or na(lastLongSigBar)  or bar_index - lastLongSigBar  > masterCd
shortCdOK = masterCd == 0 or na(lastShortSigBar) or bar_index - lastShortSigBar > masterCd

baseLongOK  = filtBias >= 0 and kzOK and volOK and sbGate and longCdOK  and (not structFilt or structDir >= 0)
baseShortOK = filtBias <= 0 and kzOK and volOK and sbGate and shortCdOK and (not structFilt or structDir <= 0)

// ---- CONFLUENCE CHRONOLOGY (V4-BUG-15) — RETIRED IN V7.5 ---------------------
// v4's gate was three global flags in any order; v5 ordered them (raid < MSS ≤
// displacement) but they were STILL the chart's latest CHoCH / CISD and "a
// displacement in the last 3 bars", none of them owned by the array being
// traded. The S/D producer now reads the MSS and displacement stamped on its
// own zone (srcMssBar / srcDispBar, MODULE 9), and the trap engine carries its
// own chronology inside the watch. Nothing global is consulted here any more.

// ---- DEDUPE (V5-11 / V4-BUG-14) ---------------------------------------------
// One liquidity event → one POI → one execution. v4 had only a bar cooldown, so
// the same pool, the same zone and the same structure shift could each produce a
// second and a third entry once masterCd bars had passed.
f_dedupeOk(int dir, int poolId, int poiId, int mssB, float poolPx) =>
    bool ok = true
    if dedupePool and poolId != 0
        ok := ok and poolId != (dir == 1 ? lastPoolIdL : lastPoolIdS)
    // V5.5-E7 · ID EQUALITY ALONE WAS NOT ENOUGH. A pool retired by age, by
    // reaction staleness or by distance leaves the registry silently, and
    // f_poolPush then re-registers the SAME price as a NEW pool with a NEW id —
    // so "one liquidity pool → one execution" quietly stopped applying to the
    // same physical level. The level is now deduped on PRICE and recency too:
    // same side, within eqTol·ATR, inside setupLife bars.
    if dedupePool and not na(poolPx)
        float lpx = dir == 1 ? lastPoolPxL : lastPoolPxS
        int   lbr = dir == 1 ? lastPoolBarL : lastPoolBarS
        if not na(lpx) and not na(lbr) and bar_index - lbr <= setupLife
            // V7.7 · F-07 · the SAME tolerance the registry fuses levels with. v7.6 used
            //   the raw `eqTol * atr` here while f_poolPush uses `lqTol`, which carries
            //   the optional eqTickMin tick floor. On a tick-quantised instrument with
            //   that floor set, the registry treated two prices as ONE pool while this
            //   dedupe treated them as two — so "one liquidity pool → one execution"
            //   quietly stopped applying to the very levels the floor exists for.
            ok := ok and math.abs(poolPx - lpx) > lqTol
    if dedupePoi and poiId != 0
        ok := ok and poiId != (dir == 1 ? lastPoiIdL : lastPoiIdS)
    if dedupeMss and not na(mssB)
        ok := ok and mssB != nz(dir == 1 ? lastMssBarL : lastMssBarS, -1)
    ok

allowConf = entryMode != "Narrative only (A+)"
allowAll  = entryMode == "All engines (legacy)"

// ---- V7.5 · §28 · STORY IDENTITY ----------------------------------------------
// One executed story can never execute again, whichever producer re-discovers
// it: direction | pool | raid bar | POI | MSS bar | FVG bar. The component
// dedupes above (pool id, pool price, POI, MSS) still apply on top of this.
var storyKeys = array.new<string>()
f_storyKey(bool go, int dir, int pid, int rb, int zid, int mb, int fb) =>
    string k = ""
    if go
        k := str.tostring(dir) + "|" + str.tostring(pid) + "|" + str.tostring(nz(rb, -1)) + "|" + str.tostring(zid) + "|" + str.tostring(nz(mb, -1)) + "|" + str.tostring(nz(fb, -1))
    k
f_storyNew(string k) => k == "" or array.indexof(storyKeys, k) < 0

// ══════════════════════════════ MODULE 14B · RISK PRIMITIVES (ARCH-7)
// These lived in v4's MODULE 16, i.e. AFTER the signal had already been emitted,
// which is exactly why v4 could print a trade whose structural stop was 3·ATR
// wide with un-raided liquidity 0.4·ATR overhead (V4-BUG-12). They are function
// definitions, so moving them above the TRADE ENGINE changes nothing except
// that the engine can now ASK before it decides.
//
// SL HIERARCHY: 1 liquidity-raid wick · 2 protected structural swing · 3 FVG
// invalidation · 4 POI edge · 5 ATR fallback. First candidate on the correct
// side AND inside [minRiskAtr, maxRiskAtr] wins.
// TP LADDER: internal liquidity → opposing FVG/OB → external liquidity, each
// with an R floor, and no target parked behind an un-raided opposing POI.
// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
f_slOk(int dir, float entry, float lvl) =>
    not na(lvl) and (dir == 1 ? lvl < entry : lvl > entry) and math.abs(entry - lvl) >= minRiskN * atr and math.abs(entry - lvl) <= maxRiskN * atr

f_sl(int dir, float entry, float lvlRaid, float lvlProt, float lvlFvg, float lvlPoi) =>
    float pad = SL_PAD_ATR * atr
    float c1 = na(lvlRaid) ? na : dir == 1 ? lvlRaid - pad : lvlRaid + pad
    float c2 = na(lvlProt) ? na : dir == 1 ? lvlProt - pad : lvlProt + pad
    float c3 = na(lvlFvg)  ? na : dir == 1 ? lvlFvg  - pad : lvlFvg  + pad
    float c4 = na(lvlPoi)  ? na : dir == 1 ? lvlPoi  - pad : lvlPoi  + pad
    float sl = na
    int   src = 5
    if f_slOk(dir, entry, c1)
        sl  := c1
        src := 1
    else if f_slOk(dir, entry, c2)
        sl  := c2
        src := 2
    else if f_slOk(dir, entry, c3)
        sl  := c3
        src := 3
    else if f_slOk(dir, entry, c4)
        sl  := c4
        src := 4
    else
