        sl  := dir == 1 ? entry - atrStopMult * atr : entry + atrStopMult * atr
        src := 5
    // V5.1-A4: the ATR fallback is NOT exempt from the risk clamp. v5 returned it
    // unchecked, so a candidate whose every structural level had been rejected FOR
    // BEING WIDER than maxRiskAtr still printed — with a 1·ATR stop sitting inside
    // the level that invalidates the thesis. That is a worse answer to V4-BUG-12
    // than the bug it replaced. `ok` is a HARD gate in MODULE 15 now.
    bool ok = src < 5 or f_slOk(dir, entry, sl)
    [sl, src, ok]

f_slTxt(int src) =>
    src == 1 ? "raid wick" : src == 2 ? "protected swing" : src == 3 ? "FVG invalidation" : src == 4 ? "POI edge" : "ATR fallback"

// nearest live pool beyond `from` in the trade direction.
// scope 0 = internal (minor swings) · 1 = external (EXT / PD / session / EQ)
// V5.4-D2: `go` gates the scan INSIDE the function (the lineage pattern —
// Pine evaluates both branches of a ternary, so gating the call would not
// skip the work). go = "a producer fired this bar, or this is the last bar".
f_nextPool(bool go, int dir, float from, int scope) =>
    float best = na
    if go and array.size(pools) > 0
        for i = 0 to array.size(pools) - 1
            Pool p = array.get(pools, i)
            bool kindOk = scope == 0 ? p.kind == PK_SWING : p.kind == PK_EXT or p.kind == PK_PD or p.kind == PK_SES or p.kind == PK_EQ or p.kind == PK_HTF or p.kind == PK_PW or p.kind == PK_TL
            bool sideOk = dir == 1 ? p.buySide and p.px > from : not p.buySide and p.px < from
            if p.state <= PS_VAL and kindOk and sideOk
                if na(best) or (dir == 1 ? p.px < best : p.px > best)
                    best := p.px
    best

// nearest OPPOSING zone edge beyond `from` — the wall a target must respect
f_nextZone(bool go, int dir, float from) =>
    float best = na
    if go and array.size(zones) > 0
        for i = 0 to array.size(zones) - 1
            Zone z = array.get(zones, i)
            if not z.signaled and z.state < 3
                if dir == 1 and z.isSupply
                    float b = z.bot             // V5.4-D1
                    if b > from and (na(best) or b < best)
                        best := b
                else if dir == -1 and not z.isSupply
                    float tp = z.top
                    if tp < from and (na(best) or tp > best)
                        best := tp
    best

// §17 · OPPOSING LIQUIDITY. The nearest MEANINGFUL thing standing between the
// entry and its target: a live pool on the opposite side that is either
// engineered / external / session / previous-day liquidity or a minor swing that
// price has already respected, or an opposing POI that is still fresh (less than
// half mitigated) and graded T1/T2. Un-validated minor pivots are deliberately
// excluded — they sit everywhere and would cap every trade at nothing.
// "Risk 10 with resistance 8 away" is not a 2R trade however the maths is drawn.
f_oppLiq(bool go, int dir, float from) =>
    float best = na
    if go and array.size(pools) > 0
        for i = 0 to array.size(pools) - 1
            Pool p = array.get(pools, i)
            bool meaty  = p.kind != PK_SWING or p.state == PS_VAL
            bool sideOk = dir == 1 ? p.buySide and p.px > from : not p.buySide and p.px < from
            if p.state <= PS_VAL and meaty and sideOk
                if na(best) or (dir == 1 ? p.px < best : p.px > best)
                    best := p.px
    if go and array.size(zones) > 0
        for i = 0 to array.size(zones) - 1
            Zone z = array.get(zones, i)
            if not z.signaled and z.state < 2 and z.tier <= 2
                if dir == 1 and z.isSupply
                    float zb = z.bot            // V5.4-D1
                    if zb > from and (na(best) or zb < best)
                        best := zb
                else if dir == -1 and not z.isSupply
                    float zt2 = z.top
                    if zt2 < from and (na(best) or zt2 > best)
                        best := zt2
    best

// V5.1-A1 · TP2 IS MEASURED, NEVER SYNTHESISED.
// v5 seeded `t2 = entry + rrMult*risk` and then computed RR as
// (t2 - entry)/risk — which is rrMult, exactly. With the defaults
// rrMult = 2.0 >= rrAplus = 2.0 every RR floor in the file passed
// unconditionally, on every bar, on every instrument. In "Next level" mode it
// was worse: f_tp never touched t2 at all, so RR was a literal constant.
// The 4th return value `s2` records WHERE TP2 came from:
//   0 = synthetic (no structural target found) → MODULE 15 blocks the trade
//   1 = opposing zone wall · 2 = external liquidity · 3 = internal liquidity
//   4 = "Next level" target · -1 = "R multiple" (the user opted out of
//       structural targeting, so the RR gate is inert BY REQUEST)
f_tp(bool go, int dir, float entry, float risk) =>
    float t1 = dir == 1 ? entry + risk : entry - risk
    float t2 = dir == 1 ? entry + rrMult * risk : entry - rrMult * risk
    float t3 = dir == 1 ? entry + 3 * risk : entry - 3 * risk
    int   s2 = 0
    if tpMode == "Liquidity ladder"
        float iL = f_nextPool(go, dir, entry, 0)
        float zW = f_nextZone(go, dir, entry)
        float eL = f_nextPool(go, dir, entry, 1)
        float minD = rrB * risk        // the loosest floor any grade can use
        if not na(iL) and (dir == 1 ? iL - entry >= risk : entry - iL >= risk)
            t1 := iL
        // nearest candidate that is at least the loosest RR floor away, by
        // preference: opposing wall → external liquidity → internal liquidity
        float c2 = na
        int   cs = 0
        if not na(zW) and (dir == 1 ? zW - entry >= minD : entry - zW >= minD)
            c2 := zW
            cs := 1
        if not na(eL) and (dir == 1 ? eL - entry >= minD : entry - eL >= minD)
            if na(c2) or (dir == 1 ? eL < c2 : eL > c2)
                c2 := eL
                cs := 2
        if na(c2) and not na(iL) and (dir == 1 ? iL - entry >= minD : entry - iL >= minD)
            c2 := iL
            cs := 3
        if not na(c2)
            t2 := c2
            s2 := cs
        // t3 is only ever promoted OUTWARD (v5 let a 2R external pool drag TP3
        // down from its 3R default)
        if not na(eL) and (dir == 1 ? eL - entry >= math.max(2 * risk, t3 - entry) : entry - eL >= math.max(2 * risk, entry - t3))
            t3 := eL
        // never park ANY rung behind an un-raided opposing POI. v5 clamped t3
        // only, so TP1 and TP2 could both sit beyond the wall, and when t2 had
        // been promoted TO the wall the clamp collapsed t3 onto t2. v5 also
        // clamped against ZONES only, while the input tooltip promised
        // "un-raided opposing pool" — the wall is the nearest of both now.
        float pW = f_oppLiq(go, dir, entry)
        float wRaw = na(zW) ? pW : na(pW) ? zW : dir == 1 ? math.min(zW, pW) : math.max(zW, pW)
        if tpClamp and not na(wRaw)
            float wall = dir == 1 ? wRaw - NEAR_LVL_ATR * atr : wRaw + NEAR_LVL_ATR * atr
            if dir == 1
                t1 := math.min(t1, wall)
                t2 := math.min(t2, wall)
                t3 := math.min(t3, wall)
            else
                t1 := math.max(t1, wall)
                t2 := math.max(t2, wall)
                t3 := math.max(t3, wall)
    else if tpMode == "Next level"
        float tgt = na
        if dir == 1
            tgt := not na(nsBot) and nsBot > entry ? nsBot : not na(lastRes) and lastRes > entry ? lastRes : na
            if not na(tgt) and tgt - entry >= rrB * risk
                t2 := tgt
                t3 := math.max(t3, tgt)
                s2 := 4
        else
            tgt := not na(nbTop) and nbTop < entry ? nbTop : not na(lastSup) and lastSup < entry ? lastSup : na
            if not na(tgt) and entry - tgt >= rrB * risk
                t2 := tgt
                t3 := math.min(t3, tgt)
                s2 := 4
    else
        s2 := -1                        // "R multiple" — synthetic by request
    // keep the ladder monotone by CAPPING t1, never by inflating t2. v5 did
    // `t2 := math.max(t2, t1)`, so an internal pool promoted to TP1 five R away
    // dragged TP2 to 5R and bought an A+ RR off a TP1 level.
    if dir == 1
        t1 := math.min(t1, t2)
        t3 := math.max(t3, t2)
    else
        t1 := math.max(t1, t2)
        t3 := math.min(t3, t2)
    [t1, t2, t3, s2]

// §15 / §16 · GRADE. HARD gates decide IF a trade exists; this decides WHAT it
// is. A+ demands the complete owned narrative, a grade-A raid, a live tier-1
// POI, a CHoCH-grade shift when it is fighting the HTF, and the A+ RR floor —
// none of which a high score can substitute for (V4-BUG-13).
f_grade(bool mach, bool narr, int tier, int rq, bool rev, bool mssCh, float rr, float room, bool cong, bool runFade, int oteQ, bool pdExt, bool apTouch, bool elite, bool hconf, bool regDown) =>
    // V5.1-A3 · REVERSAL IS A HARD BLOCK, NOT A DOWNGRADE.
    // The header and the allowRev tooltip both promise a counter-HTF setup needs
    // a grade-A raid, a tier-1 POI and a CHoCH-grade MSS. v5 coded
    // `revOk = not rev or mssCh` — one of the three — and a failure only pushed
    // the setup down to grade B, which trades at the default minGrade. So the
    // advertised reversal requirements never blocked anything.
    // V7.0 · §10 · a REVERSAL must also come from the right half of the range.
    // The HTF premium / discount extreme is already required at the context
    // stage; this adds the CHART dealing range, because a counter-trend entry
    // taken at equilibrium is the one trade premium / discount exists to refuse.
    bool revOk = not rev or (mssCh and tier == 1 and rq == RQ_A and pdExt)
    int  g     = GR_NONE
    if revOk
        // V5.1-A2 · GRADE B NOW HAS EVIDENCE REQUIREMENTS.
        // v5 opened with `int g = GR_B` unconditionally, so a candidate with a
        // tier-3 POI, no graded raid, no narrative and no structural target still
        // graded B and cleared the default minGrade — hGrade was a no-op and
        // "hard gates a high score can never buy past" was untrue by default.
        if rq >= RQ_C and f_geq(rr, rrB)
            g := GR_B
        if g == GR_B and (not mach or narr) and tier <= 2 and rq >= RQ_B and f_geq(rr, rrAn)
            g := GR_A
        // §11 / §16 · A+ is the complete narrative, and "price returned to a
        //     meaningful institutional level" is part of that narrative. With
        //     oteNeedAp on, an entry that never retraced into its own leg's
        //     62 % is an A, not an A+, however good everything else looks.
        // V7.1 · THREE CHANGES TO THE A+ RUNG, two of them restrictions.
        //   ① the OTE band must be the PRIME 62-70 %. A 70-79 % entry is a deep
        //     discount — tradable and scored, but capped at grade A, and past 79 %
        //     there is barely a leg left to retrace into. Both caps hold even with
        //     oteNeedAp OFF: that switch forgives NOT REACHING the OTE (oteQ 0), it
        //     does not promote a retracement that ran too deep.
        //   ② the execution array must still be UNMITIGATED: A+ only within
        //     apMaxTouch discrete touches of the gap (§48). Beyond that the entry
        //     still prints, at A.
        //   ③ an ELITE trap substitutes for the machine narrative, because it has
        //     a complete one of its own (f_trapElite).
        if g == GR_A and ((mach and narr) or elite) and tier == 1 and rq == RQ_A and f_geq(rr, rrAPn) and apTouch and (elite ? oteQ >= 1 : oteNeedAp ? oteQ == 1 : oteQ <= 1)
            g := GR_AP
        // Not enough room to the first opposing pool = ONE step down, and one
        // step below B is no trade. v5 made GR_B a terminal absorber, so a room
        // violation on a B candidate was forgiven entirely. RR is no longer
        // folded into this (see MODULE 15) — the two gates are independent now.
        if useOppLiq and not oppLiqHard and not na(room) and room < oppLiqMult
            g := g == GR_AP ? GR_A : g == GR_A ? GR_B : GR_NONE
        // V5.5-E5 · CONGESTION is a one-step downgrade in soft mode (§29); the
        // hard-block variant is a gate in MODULE 15, not a grade modifier.
        if cong and not congHard
            g := g == GR_AP ? GR_A : g == GR_A ? GR_B : GR_NONE
        // V5.5-E4 · FADING AN UNEXHAUSTED LIQUIDITY RUN, same treatment (§11).
        if runFade and runGate == "Downgrade"
            g := g == GR_AP ? GR_A : g == GR_A ? GR_B : GR_NONE
        // V7.5 · §9 · HTF CONFLICT (soft mode) and §24 · REGIME POLICY, one step
        //   each. Their hard modes are gates in MODULE 15, not grade modifiers.
        if hconf and htfConfMode == "Downgrade"
            g := g == GR_AP ? GR_A : g == GR_A ? GR_B : GR_NONE
        if regDown
            g := g == GR_AP ? GR_A : g == GR_A ? GR_B : GR_NONE
    g

// ═════════════════════════════════════ MODULE 15 · TRADE ENGINE (ARCH-6)
// The ONLY authority that may declare LONG / SHORT. The order of business is
// now the one §24 asks for:
//
//   producer selection → SL / TP / RR / opposing liquidity → HARD GATES →
//   soft score → grade → arbitration → consumption
//
// Exactly one producer per direction wins by RANK (FEM > machine > S/D retest >
// CRT > turtle soup > trendline sweep > price action). If both directions
// qualify on one bar the higher-ranked one wins and an exact tie mutes BOTH.
// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// V6.5-O4 · ONE PRODUCER CHAIN, TWO CALLS.
// v6.4 spelled the identical five-producer if / else-if ladder out twice — once
// per direction — preceded by forty default declarations. Every branch, every
// rank, every tag and every field write below is byte-for-byte what the LONG and
// SHORT blocks did; only the direction-specific NAMES became parameters, and the
// two strings that genuinely differ ("Demand / Supply 50% retest" and
// "① BEAR / BULL TRAP") are chosen from `isL`. The defaults that used to be the
// forty declarations are now the function's own locals, and the destructures
// below declare the same forty globals with the same types.
// V7.5 · NO anyLo* / anyHi* ARGUMENT REACHES THIS FUNCTION. The machine reads
// its Setup, the S/D producer reads its ZONE's stamped chain, the trap reads
// its WATCH. Every value below is owned by the object that produced the entry.
f_prod(bool isL, bool mach, Setup s, bool sdC, Zone sdZ, int sdRetQ, bool pa1, float paSl, bool isRev, Trap tw, int tq, bool tel) =>
    int    sdPid = f_zoneSrcPool(sdZ)       // na-safe: 0 without a candidate
    int    sdBnd = f_zoneBound(sdZ)         // V7.7 F-10 · likewise
    int    pr   = 0
    string tg   = ""
    float  psl  = na
    int    ti   = 3
    bool   fr   = false
    int    rqv  = 0
    int    rk   = 0
    bool   rv   = false
    bool   mc   = false
    int    dq   = 0
    int    mb   = na
    int    dbr  = na
    int    pid  = 0
    int    zid  = 0
    int    rb   = na
    int    fb   = na
    int    oq   = 0
    bool   idm  = false
    float  fvA  = 0
    string etag = ""
    float  epx  = na
    if mach
        pr   := s.poiIsHtf ? PR_FEM : PR_MACH
        tg   := s.poiIsHtf ? "◎ FEM " + htfTf + "→LTF" : "Sweep+POI+MSS"
        psl  := s.liqExt
        ti   := s.poiTier
        fr   := s.poiFresh
        rqv  := s.liqQual
        rk   := s.liqKind
        rv   := s.reversal
        mc   := s.mssChoch
        dq   := s.dispQ
        mb   := s.mssBar
        dbr  := s.dispBar
        pid  := s.liqId
        zid  := s.poiId
        rb   := s.liqBar
        fb   := s.fvgBar
        oq   := s.oteQ
        idm  := s.induced
        fvA  := nz(s.fvgH, 0) / atr
        etag := s.liqTag
        epx  := f_poolPx(s.pool)
    // V7.5 · §6 · the S/D producer trades its zone's OWN chain: the raid that built
    //   the array (stop = its wick, grade = its grade, class = its class), the MSS
    //   after it, the displacement that left it, the inducement before it and the
    //   leg it made (OTE). sdC already proves the chain exists (MODULE 9).
    //   V7.4 F-14 · it stands down while the same-side machine works the same pool.
    //   V7.7 · F-10 · SAME POOL WAS ONLY HALF THE CONFLICT. v7.6 stood the S/D
    //     producer down when the machine was mid-narrative on the same POOL, and that
    //     is the case F-14 measured — but the collision that actually costs a trade is
    //     the same ARRAY. A machine setup binds its POI at stage 4 off the raid of pool
    //     P; that zone's own stamped chain may name a different pool Q, so the S/D
    //     producer could fire on it through Q, set signaled, and kill a stage 4-7
    //     narrative that outranks it (f_zoneDead: "POI spent"). A lower-ranked producer
    //     destroying a higher-ranked one in progress is exactly the arbitration this
    //     module exists to prevent. Zone.bound (F-02) already records the holder, so the
    //     rule costs one comparison: same pool OR same array, either one stands it down.
    else if allowConf and sdC and not (machFirst and s.st >= ST_POI and s.st < ST_ENTRY and (s.liqId == sdPid or sdBnd == s.id))
        pr   := PR_SD
        tg   := isL ? "Demand 50% retest" : "Supply 50% retest"
        psl  := sdZ.srcRaidExt
        ti   := sdZ.tier
        fr   := f_zoneFresh(sdZ)
        rqv  := sdZ.srcRaidRq
        rk   := sdZ.srcRaidKind
        rv   := isRev
        mc   := sdZ.srcMssCh
        dq   := sdZ.srcDispQ
        mb   := sdZ.srcMssBar
        dbr  := sdZ.srcDispBar
        pid  := sdPid
        zid  := sdZ.id
        rb   := sdZ.srcRaidBar
        fb   := sdZ.bornBar
        // §22 · OTE on the ZONE's own leg: raid wick → leg extreme, deepest pullback
        oq   := f_inOte(isL, isL ? sdZ.srcRaidExt : sdZ.legExt, isL ? sdZ.legExt : sdZ.srcRaidExt, sdZ.deepest, close)
        idm  := sdZ.srcIdm
        fvA  := (sdZ.top - sdZ.bot) / atr
        etag := sdZ.srcRaidTag
        epx  := sdZ.srcRaidPx
    // V7.1 · an ELITE ① trap is admitted in EVERY entry mode, "Narrative only"
    //   included, because it brings its own complete causal chain. Ordinary ①
    //   traps still need "All engines". V7.4 F-04 · its identity is the pool it
    //   BROKE (wId / wPx / wKnd), its chronology lives in the watch.
    //   V7.5 · honest trigger block: the trap has NO MSS of its own and no impulse
    //   leg, so it earns displacement points only when its reclaim candle displaced,
    //   and reads OTE / inducement / touch history as unknown, not as somebody else's.
    //   V7.6 · F-08 · the watch is now a per-side object, passed in as `tw` with its
    //   own grade and elite verdict, so the two sides no longer share one slot.
    else if (allowAll or tel) and pa1
        pr   := PR_TRAP
        tg   := (isL ? "① BEAR TRAP " : "① BULL TRAP ") + f_rqTxt(tq) + (tel ? "+" : "")
        psl  := paSl
        ti   := tel ? 1 : tq >= RQ_B ? 2 : 3
        rqv  := tq
        rk   := tw.knd
        rv   := isRev
        dq   := (isL ? dispUpBar : dispDnBar) ? (isL ? dqUp : dqDn) : 0
        dbr  := bar_index
        pid  := tw.id
        rb   := tw.sbar
        etag := tw.knd < 0 ? "LEVEL" : f_kindTxt(tw.knd)
        epx  := tw.px
    [pr, tg, psl, ti, fr, rqv, rk, rv, mc, dq, mb, dbr, pid, zid, rb, fb, oq, idm, fvA, etag, epx]

// ---- LONG candidate selection, best rank first ------------------------------
[prodL, tagL, prodSlL, tierL, freshL, rqL, rkindL, revL, mssChL, dispQL, mssBL, dispBL, poolIdL, poiIdL, raidBL, fvgBL, oteQL, indHitL, fvgAtrL, evTagL, evPxL] = f_prod(true, machLong, upS, sdCandLong, sdZoneL, sdRetQL, pa1Long, paSlLong, htfIsRevLong, trapD, trapQL, trapElL)

// ---- SHORT candidate selection ----------------------------------------------
[prodS, tagS, prodSlS, tierS, freshS, rqS, rkindS, revS, mssChS, dispQS, mssBS, dispBS, poolIdS, poiIdS, raidBS, fvgBS, oteQS, indHitS, fvgAtrS, evTagS, evPxS] = f_prod(false, machShort, dnS, sdCandShort, sdZoneS, sdRetQS, pa1Short, paSlShort, htfIsRevShort, trapU, trapQS, trapElS)

// ---- V7.9 · RECLAIM R1 · FIVE f_prod SLOTS WERE DERIVABLE FROM `prod` --------
//   `pr` already encodes which producer won, and f_prod is INLINED TWICE, so a
//   field that can be re-derived here costs a declaration, a write and a tuple
//   slot twice over for nothing. Each line reproduces its old slot EXACTLY,
//   including the no-producer case (f_prod returned the default when pr == 0,
//   and the `prodX > 0` guard below returns the same default).
//     hp  · pr := poiIsHtf ? PR_FEM : PR_MACH, so PR_FEM IS "the POI is the HTF array"
//     mo  · was true for the machine and the S/D producer, false for the trap
//     rtq · the machine's own retest quality, the S/D candidate's measured depth, 0
//     fvgE / poiE · written only by the machine (and poiE also by S/D, from the
//           very zone `sdBotLong` / `sdTopShort` were taken from in MODULE 9)
htfPoiL = prodL == PR_FEM
htfPoiS = prodS == PR_FEM
mssOkL  = prodL > 0 and prodL < PR_TRAP
mssOkS  = prodS > 0 and prodS < PR_TRAP
retQL   = prodL > 0 and prodL <= PR_MACH ? upS.retestQ : prodL == PR_SD ? sdRetQL : 0
retQS   = prodS > 0 and prodS <= PR_MACH ? dnS.retestQ : prodS == PR_SD ? sdRetQS : 0
fvgBotL = prodL > 0 and prodL <= PR_MACH ? upS.fvgBot : na
fvgTopS = prodS > 0 and prodS <= PR_MACH ? dnS.fvgTop : na
poiBotL = prodL > 0 and prodL <= PR_MACH ? upS.poiBot : prodL == PR_SD ? sdBotLong : na
poiTopS = prodS > 0 and prodS <= PR_MACH ? dnS.poiTop : prodS == PR_SD ? sdTopShort : na

// ---- V5.5-E · WHOSE EVENT IS THIS? (§7 / §8 / E4) --------------------------
// V7.5 · resolved INSIDE f_prod from the producing object (evTag / evPx / pid).
// The descriptor list lives on the pool; a zone's pool may have left the
// registry, in which case the S/D entry simply carries no descriptors.
trapL  = prodL == PR_TRAP
trapS  = prodS == PR_TRAP
evIdL  = poolIdL
evIdS  = poolIdS
Pool evPoolL = prodL <= PR_MACH ? upS.pool : f_poolById(prodL == PR_SD, poolIdL)
Pool evPoolS = prodS <= PR_MACH ? dnS.pool : f_poolById(prodS == PR_SD, poolIdS)
descTL = f_poolDesc(evPoolL)
descTS = f_poolDesc(evPoolS)
sbWinL = prodL <= PR_MACH ? upS.sbWindow : sbActive
sbWinS = prodS <= PR_MACH ? dnS.sbWindow : sbActive
sesStoryL = prodL > 0 and f_sesStory(evTagL, rkindL)
sesStoryS = prodS > 0 and f_sesStory(evTagS, rkindS)

// ---- RISK FIRST (V4-BUG-12). Both sides are resolved unconditionally at top
//      level because Pine keeps tuple destructuring out of conditional scopes.
//      V5.4-D2: the ladder / room SCANS inside them only run when a producer
//      fired or on the last bar (dashboard / debug). okLong / okShort already
//      require prodX > 0, so a skipped bar's na values can gate nothing.
needRiskL = prodL > 0 or barstate.islast
needRiskS = prodS > 0 or barstate.islast
// V7.4 E2-C · the ENTRY PRICE: the rejection close (v7.3), or the level actually
// traded — the execution gap's 50 % (CE) for the machine, the zone midpoint for
// the S/D producer. Every stop, target, room and RR figure below reads it.
ceMode   = entryModel != "Rejection close (v7.3)"
// V7.6 · F-02 · A CE ENTRY IS ONLY HONEST IF THE SIGNAL BAR TRADED THAT LEVEL.
//   The machine's gap 50 % is reachable at retestDepth >= 0.5 (stage 8 demands
//   `low <= fvgTop - retestDepth * h`) and NOT below it — the input allows 0.0.
//   The S/D zone's midpoint is worse: its 50 % tap may be up to confirmWin bars old,
//   so the signal bar only has to touch the zone's near edge and print a rejection.
//   In both cases v7.5 then measured risk, RR, room and the whole TP ladder from a
//   fill that was never available, and RR was overstated in exactly the direction
//   that passes hRr and the per-grade floors. The CE level is used only when this
//   bar's range contains it; otherwise the rejection close is the honest entry.
ceRawL   = prodL > 0 and prodL <= PR_MACH ? upS.fvgTop - 0.5 * nz(upS.fvgH, 0) : prodL == PR_SD ? (sdTopLong + sdBotLong) / 2 : na
ceRawS   = prodS > 0 and prodS <= PR_MACH ? dnS.fvgBot + 0.5 * nz(dnS.fvgH, 0) : prodS == PR_SD ? (sdTopShort + sdBotShort) / 2 : na
entryPxL = ceMode and not na(ceRawL) and ceRawL >= low and ceRawL <= high ? ceRawL : close
entryPxS = ceMode and not na(ceRawS) and ceRawS >= low and ceRawS <= high ? ceRawS : close
lvlProtL = nz(protLowI, protLowE)
// V5.5-E3 · NO CROSS-PRODUCER POI STOP. PR_SD already writes poiBotL from
// sdBotLong, so v5.4's `: sdBotLong` fallback could ONLY ever fire for a
// producer that has no POI at all (CRT / turtle soup / trap) — and it then handed
// f_sl an unrelated demand zone's edge as stop candidate 4, i.e. a stop sitting
// inside the level that invalidates a different thesis. A producer with no POI
// now simply has no POI candidate.
[slL, slSrcL, slOkL] = f_sl(1, entryPxL, prodL > 0 ? prodSlL : na, lvlProtL, fvgBotL, poiBotL)
riskL = math.max(math.abs(entryPxL - slL), syminfo.mintick)
[tp1L, tp2L, tp3L, tgtSrcL] = f_tp(needRiskL, 1, entryPxL, riskL)
oppL   = f_oppLiq(needRiskL, 1, entryPxL)
// na room = "nothing standing in the way", NOT 99.0 (v5's sentinel leaked into
// the debug panel as "opposing liquidity only 99.00R away").
roomL  = na(oppL) ? na : (oppL - entryPxL) / riskL
// V5.1-A5: RR is the PURE measured reward-to-risk to TP2. v5 folded the room
// check into it with math.min(rrRaw, room), which meant the documented soft mode
// (oppLiqHard = OFF) hard-blocked anyway whenever oppLiqMult <= rrB — and did it
// through the RR gate, so the debug panel reported the wrong reason. Room is a
// gate and a grade modifier now; it is not arithmetic on RR.
rrL    = (tp2L - entryPxL) / riskL

lvlProtS = nz(protHighI, protHighE)
[slS, slSrcS, slOkS] = f_sl(-1, entryPxS, prodS > 0 ? prodSlS : na, lvlProtS, fvgTopS, poiTopS)
riskS = math.max(math.abs(entryPxS - slS), syminfo.mintick)
[tp1S, tp2S, tp3S, tgtSrcS] = f_tp(needRiskS, -1, entryPxS, riskS)
oppS   = f_oppLiq(needRiskS, -1, entryPxS)
roomS  = na(oppS) ? na : (entryPxS - oppS) / riskS
rrS    = (entryPxS - tp2S) / riskS

// ---- V7.0 · the ingredients the machine OWNS, resolved per side -------------
// V7.5 · inducement (indHitL/S) and the OTE band (oteQL/S) come out of f_prod:
// the machine's own leg, the S/D zone's own leg, and UNKNOWN (0 / false) for the
// trap — v7.4 handed non-machine producers the chart-wide readings instead.
// V7.1 · the remaining three ingredients are read exactly once each, so they are
// spelled at their call sites rather than named here:
//   §48 A+ needs an UNMITIGATED array — `prodX > PR_MACH or Xs.fvgTouches <= apMaxTouch`
//   §2  did this setup's own displacement leave the dealing range — `Xs.dispOutRng`
//   §15 an ELITE trap carries the machine's A+ privilege — `prodX == PR_TRAP and trapEl`

// ---- SOFT SCORE of the winning candidate of each side -----------------------
// V7.6 · F-12 · the S/D producer HAS a touch history and was not asking for it. f_prod
//   left `tc` at 0 for PR_SD because the zone stores no touch count, but the pool it
//   owns is resolved by identity two lines above (evPoolL) — so the producer was
//   forfeiting up to 3 of its 25 LIQUIDITY points whenever its pool was still tracked,
//   a systematic ranking bias against it rather than an honest unknown.
[evL, scoreLong]  = f_score(1,  tierL, htfPoiL, freshL, retQL, zoneBullConf or bullPin, rqL, rkindL, prodL > 0 ? f_poolTouch(evPoolL) : 0, revL, mssChL, mssOkL, dispQL, mssBL, dispBL, fvgAtrL, sesStoryL, f_poolDescN(evPoolL), congMeasured, indHitL, oteQL, atHtfPoiL, prodL <= PR_MACH and upS.dispOutRng)
[evS, scoreShort] = f_score(-1, tierS, htfPoiS, freshS, retQS, zoneBearConf or bearPin, rqS, rkindS, prodS > 0 ? f_poolTouch(evPoolS) : 0, revS, mssChS, mssOkS, dispQS, mssBS, dispBS, fvgAtrS, sesStoryS, f_poolDescN(evPoolS), congMeasured, indHitS, oteQS, atHtfPoiS, prodS <= PR_MACH and dnS.dispOutRng)

// ---- V7.5 · §24 · REGIME → EXECUTION POLICY -----------------------------------
// TREND prefers continuation (a reversal costs a grade) · RANGE prefers the
// correct half (equilibrium-side entries cost a grade) · EXPANSION never chases
// (the trap's reclaim-close entry is a hard refusal below) · CHOP = the
// congestion gate (Downgrade / A+ only / Block).
regDownL = regPolicy and ((regime == RG_TREND and revL) or (regime == RG_RANGE and not discOK))
regDownS = regPolicy and ((regime == RG_TREND and revS) or (regime == RG_RANGE and not premOK))

// ---- GRADE -------------------------------------------------------------------
gradeL = prodL == 0 ? GR_NONE : f_grade(prodL <= PR_MACH, narrLong,  tierL, rqL, revL, mssChL, rrL, roomL, congested, runFadeLong, oteQL, inDiscExt, prodL > PR_MACH or upS.fvgTouches <= apMaxTouch, prodL == PR_TRAP and trapElL, htfConf, regDownL)
gradeS = prodS == 0 ? GR_NONE : f_grade(prodS <= PR_MACH, narrShort, tierS, rqS, revS, mssChS, rrS, roomS, congested, runFadeShort, oteQS, inPremExt, prodS > PR_MACH or dnS.fvgTouches <= apMaxTouch, prodS == PR_TRAP and trapElS, htfConf, regDownS)
aPlusLong  = gradeL == GR_AP
aPlusShort = gradeS == GR_AP

// ---- HARD GATES (§16). A high score can never buy past any of these ---------
// hSl  — a stop exists INSIDE the risk clamp (V5.1-A4). v5 had no such gate.
// hTgt — TP2 came from a real level, not from rrMult (V5.1-A1).
// hRr  — the loosest floor only; the PER-GRADE floors are preconditions inside
//        f_grade, so testing them again here (against the already-downgraded
//        grade, as v5 did) could only ever loosen them.
hNarrL  = prodL > PR_MACH or narrLong
hTgtL   = tgtSrcL != 0
hRrL    = f_geq(rrL, rrB)
hRoomL  = not useOppLiq or not oppLiqHard or na(roomL) or roomL >= oppLiqMult
hDedupL = f_dedupeOk(1, poolIdL, poiIdL, mssBL, evPxL)
hTierL  = tierL <= minTier
hGradeL = gradeL >= f_grMin(minGrade)
hScoreL = minScore == 0 or scoreLong >= minScore
// V5.5-E4 / E5 · the two new hard gates. In "Downgrade" mode both are grade
// modifiers inside f_grade and these evaluate true; only "Block" makes them hard.
hCongL  = not congested or not congHard or (congGate == "A+ only" and gradeL == GR_AP)
hRunL   = not runFadeLong or runGate != "Block"
// V7.5 · §28 story identity · §9 HTF conflict (hard modes) · §24 regime policy
storyL  = f_storyKey(prodL > 0, 1, poolIdL, raidBL, poiIdL, mssBL, fvgBL)
hStoryL = f_storyNew(storyL)
hHtfL   = not htfConf or htfConfMode == "Off" or htfConfMode == "Downgrade" or (htfConfMode == "A+ only" and gradeL == GR_AP)
hRegL   = not (regPolicy and regime == RG_EXP and trapL)
okLong  = prodL > 0 and baseLongOK and hNarrL and slOkL and hTgtL and hRrL and hRoomL and hDedupL and hTierL and hCongL and hRunL and hGradeL and hScoreL and hStoryL and hHtfL and hRegL

hNarrS  = prodS > PR_MACH or narrShort
hTgtS   = tgtSrcS != 0
hRrS    = f_geq(rrS, rrB)
hRoomS  = not useOppLiq or not oppLiqHard or na(roomS) or roomS >= oppLiqMult
hDedupS = f_dedupeOk(-1, poolIdS, poiIdS, mssBS, evPxS)
hTierS  = tierS <= minTier
hGradeS = gradeS >= f_grMin(minGrade)
hScoreS = minScore == 0 or scoreShort >= minScore
hCongS  = not congested or not congHard or (congGate == "A+ only" and gradeS == GR_AP)
hRunS   = not runFadeShort or runGate != "Block"
storyS  = f_storyKey(prodS > 0, -1, poolIdS, raidBS, poiIdS, mssBS, fvgBS)
hStoryS = f_storyNew(storyS)
hHtfS   = not htfConf or htfConfMode == "Off" or htfConfMode == "Downgrade" or (htfConfMode == "A+ only" and gradeS == GR_AP)
hRegS   = not (regPolicy and regime == RG_EXP and trapS)
okShort = prodS > 0 and baseShortOK and hNarrS and slOkS and hTgtS and hRrS and hRoomS and hDedupS and hTierS and hCongS and hRunS and hGradeS and hScoreS and hStoryS and hHtfS and hRegS

// ---- direction arbitration: never both, never twice -------------------------
bool bothTie = okLong and okShort and prodL == prodS
finalLong  = okLong  and not bothTie and (not okShort or prodL < prodS)
finalShort = okShort and not bothTie and (not okLong  or prodS < prodL)

// ---- the exact reason an entry did NOT print, for the debug panel -----------
// V5.5-E8 · `go` short-circuits the whole chain. v5.4 rebuilt both of these
// strings — about twenty str.tostring calls and their concatenations — on EVERY
// historical bar, while the only consumer is the debug table on the last bar.
// Pine evaluates both branches of a ternary, so wrapping the CALL would not have
// skipped the work; the gate has to be inside, which is the same pattern Phase D
// used for the risk scanners.
f_blockReason(bool go, bool prodOk, string machBlk, bool narr, bool cdOk, bool baseOk, bool tier, int tierV, bool slOk, bool tgt, bool rr, float rrV, bool room, float roomV, bool dedup, bool grade, int g, bool rev, bool score, int scoreV, bool tie, bool cong, bool runF, bool story, bool hconf, bool reg) =>
    not go      ? "" :
         not prodOk  ? (machBlk == "" ? "no producer" : machBlk) :
         not narr   ? "narrative integrity failed (causal order / ownership)" :
         not cdOk   ? "cooldown" :
         not dedup  ? "duplicate — this pool / POI / MSS already traded" :
         not story  ? "duplicate STORY — this exact narrative (pool · raid · POI · MSS · FVG) already executed" :
         not baseOk ? "context filter (bias / KZ / volatility / SB / structure)" :
         not tier   ? "POI tier " + f_tierTxt(tierV) + " below the minimum" :
         not slOk   ? "no stop inside the risk clamp (" + str.tostring(minRiskN, "#.##") + "–" + str.tostring(maxRiskN, "#.##") + " ATR)" :
         not tgt    ? "no structural target ≥ " + str.tostring(rrB, "#.##") + "R away — TP2 would be synthetic" :
         not rr     ? "RR " + str.tostring(rrV, "#.##") + " below the absolute floor " + str.tostring(rrB, "#.##") :
         not room   ? "opposing liquidity only " + f_roomTxt(roomV) + " away" :
         not cong   ? "congestion — " + str.tostring(congN) + "/4 chop readings in the last " + str.tostring(congLen) + " bars" :
         not runF   ? "would fade an unexhausted liquidity run (" + str.tostring(run.legs) + " legs, " + f_rnTxt(run.cls) + ")" :
         not hconf  ? "HTF CONFLICT — structure " + f_biasTxt(hStrDir) + " vs momentum " + f_biasTxt(htfMom) + " (" + htfConfMode + ")" :
         not reg    ? "regime policy — EXPANSION: a reclaim-close entry is a chase" :
         not grade  ? (g == GR_NONE and rev ? "REVERSAL setup without a grade-A raid + tier-1 POI + CHoCH + a premium/discount extreme" :
              g == GR_NONE ? "no grade — insufficient evidence (raid quality / RR)" :
              "grade " + f_grTxt(g) + " below the minimum") :
         not score  ? "score " + str.tostring(scoreV) + " < " + str.tostring(minScore) :
         tie        ? "equal-rank conflict — both sides muted" : ""

dbgOn = showDebug and barstate.islast
blockLong  = finalLong  ? "" : f_blockReason(dbgOn, prodL > 0, blkLong,  hNarrL, longCdOK,  baseLongOK,  hTierL, tierL, slOkL, hTgtL, hRrL, rrL, hRoomL, roomL, hDedupL, hGradeL, gradeL, revL, hScoreL, scoreLong,  bothTie, hCongL, hRunL, hStoryL, hHtfL, hRegL)
blockShort = finalShort ? "" : f_blockReason(dbgOn, prodS > 0, blkShort, hNarrS, shortCdOK, baseShortOK, hTierS, tierS, slOkS, hTgtS, hRrS, rrS, hRoomS, roomS, hDedupS, hGradeS, gradeS, revS, hScoreS, scoreShort, bothTie, hCongS, hRunS, hStoryS, hHtfS, hRegS)

// ---- CONSUMPTION: state is spent only now, and only by the winner ----------
if finalLong
    lastLongSigBar := bar_index
    lastPoolIdL    := poolIdL
    lastPoiIdL     := poiIdL
    lastMssBarL    := mssBL
    lastPoolPxL    := evPxL      // V5.5-E7
    lastPoolBarL   := bar_index
    // V7.5 · §28 · the executed story, 60-slot ring
    if storyL != ""
        array.push(storyKeys, storyL)
        if array.size(storyKeys) > 60
            array.shift(storyKeys)
    if prodL <= PR_MACH
        upS.st := ST_DEAD
        if not na(upS.pool)
            upS.pool.state := PS_DONE
            upS.pool.spent := true
        if not na(upS.poiZone)
            upS.poiZone.signaled := true
    else if prodL == PR_SD and not na(sdZoneL)
        sdZoneL.signaled := true
        box.set_bgcolor(sdZoneL.bx, color.new(COL_BULL, 95))
        // V7.4 F-01 / V7.5 · spend the pool the zone OWNS, looked up by identity
        //   (it may already have left the registry — then the price dedupe holds).
        Pool spL = f_poolById(dedupePool, poolIdL)
        if not na(spL)
            spL.spent := true
    else if prodL == PR_TRAP
        trapD.st := 0
if finalShort
    lastShortSigBar := bar_index
    lastPoolIdS     := poolIdS
    lastPoiIdS      := poiIdS
    lastMssBarS     := mssBS
    lastPoolPxS     := evPxS     // V5.5-E7
    lastPoolBarS    := bar_index
    if storyS != ""
        array.push(storyKeys, storyS)
        if array.size(storyKeys) > 60
            array.shift(storyKeys)
    if prodS <= PR_MACH
        dnS.st := ST_DEAD
        if not na(dnS.pool)
            dnS.pool.state := PS_DONE
            dnS.pool.spent := true
        if not na(dnS.poiZone)
            dnS.poiZone.signaled := true
    else if prodS == PR_SD and not na(sdZoneS)
        sdZoneS.signaled := true
        box.set_bgcolor(sdZoneS.bx, color.new(COL_BEAR, 95))
        Pool spS = f_poolById(dedupePool, poolIdS)
        if not na(spS)
            spS.spent := true
    else if prodS == PR_TRAP
        trapU.st := 0

// V5.2-B3 · A TRANSIENT block does not destroy the narrative. A machine setup
// that reached state 8 but was refused by cooldown / session / volatility /
// RR / room / target / grade / score / arbitration returns to state 7 and may
// retest again inside retestWin. PERMANENT verdicts stay terminal — failed
// narrative integrity and dedupe (this pool / POI / MSS already traded) leave
// the setup in state 8 for the state-9 cleanup to reset on the next bar, as
// before. The FVG lifecycle checks (traded-through, window) re-apply next bar.
if machLong and not finalLong and upS.st == ST_ENTRY and hNarrL and hDedupL
    upS.st        := ST_FVG
    upS.retestBar := na
    upS.retestQ   := 0
if machShort and not finalShort and dnS.st == ST_ENTRY and hNarrS and hDedupS
    dnS.st        := ST_FVG
    dnS.retestBar := na
    dnS.retestQ   := 0

// ---- §28 · ENTRY REASON CODE ------------------------------------------------
// One line an operator can read back: grade | what was raided and how well →
// what it reacted from → how it shifted → what it re-entered on → the RR.
f_reason(bool go, int dir, int prod, string tag, int g, int tier, bool htfPoi, string liqTag, int rq, float rr, int retQ, bool rev, bool sb, bool story, bool mssCh, int evId, string desc, bool cong, bool runF, bool idm, bool ote, int pa) =>
    bool isL = dir == 1
    string r = ""
    if go
        r := f_grTxt(g) + " | EVENT #" + str.tostring(evId) + " " + (isL ? "SSL" : "BSL") + " RAID " + f_rqTxt(rq) + (liqTag != "" ? " (" + liqTag + ")" : "")
        r += desc != "" ? " [" + desc + "]" : ""
        r += idm ? " · IDM taken" : ""
        r += " → " + (htfPoi ? "HTF " + htfTf + " POI" : prod <= PR_MACH ? f_paTxt(pa) : isL ? "DEMAND" : "SUPPLY") + " " + f_tierTxt(tier)
        r += " → " + (prod <= PR_MACH ? (mssCh ? "CHoCH" : "CISD") + " → DISP → FVG " + (retQ >= 3 ? "deep" : retQ == 2 ? "50%" : "touch") : tag)
        r += ote ? " → OTE" : ""
        r += " → " + str.tostring(rr, "#.##") + "R"
        r += rev ? " · REVERSAL" : ""
        r += story ? " · session story" : ""
        r += sb ? " · SB" : ""
        r += cong ? " · CONGESTED" : ""
        r += runF ? " · fading a run" : ""
    r

reasonLong  = f_reason(finalLong  and showReason, 1,  prodL, tagL, gradeL, tierL, htfPoiL, evTagL, rqL, rrL, retQL, revL, sbWinL, sesStoryL, mssChL, evIdL, descTL, congested, runFadeLong, indHitL, oteQL > 0, upS.poiPa)
reasonShort = f_reason(finalShort and showReason, -1, prodS, tagS, gradeS, tierS, htfPoiS, evTagS, rqS, rrS, retQS, revS, sbWinS, sesStoryS, mssChS, evIdS, descTS, congested, runFadeShort, indHitS, oteQS > 0, dnS.poiPa)

// ═══════════════════════════════════════ MODULE 16 · EMITTED RISK (ARCH-7)
// Every level was already resolved in MODULE 15, because the RR and the
// opposing-liquidity room are HARD GATES there. This module only selects the
// winning side's numbers for the drawing layer.
// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
sigDir   = finalLong ? 1 : finalShort ? -1 : 0
sigEntry = finalLong ? entryPxL : finalShort ? entryPxS : na
sigSL    = finalLong ? slL : finalShort ? slS : na
sigSlSrc = finalLong ? slSrcL : finalShort ? slSrcS : 0
sigTP1   = finalLong ? tp1L : finalShort ? tp1S : na
sigTP2   = finalLong ? tp2L : finalShort ? tp2S : na
sigTP3   = finalLong ? tp3L : finalShort ? tp3S : na

// ════════════════════════ MODULE 16C · TRADE MANAGEMENT (V7.8)
// WHAT WAS MISSING. Every release of this lineage stopped at the entry. It
// resolved a stop and a three-rung target ladder, drew them, and then had no
// further opinion: no break-even rule, no record of which rung was reached, and
// not one alert for any of it. An operator running the file on alerts learned
// when to enter and never learned anything else.
//
// WHAT THIS IS, AND WHAT IT IS NOT. It is BOOKKEEPING on the LAST emitted
// signal. It places no order, sizes no position and manages no risk for you.
// Above all it is DOWNSTREAM: nothing in this module is read by a producer, a
// gate, a grade, the score, the dedupe, the registry or either state machine, so
// it cannot change which trades the engine emits. That separation is the same
// one MODULE 17 keeps, and it is why this can be added without re-auditing the
// engine above it.
//
// THE THREE RULES THAT KEEP IT HONEST
//  1 · THE SIGNAL BAR IS NOT EVALUATED. Tracking starts on the bar AFTER the
//      entry. The signal candle's own range is what produced the rejection the
//      entry was taken on, so letting it also reach a target would be the same
//      class of error as V4-BUG-6/7 — an event confirming itself.
//  2 · THE STOP IS ASSUMED FIRST. When one candle's range contains both the stop
//      and a target, OHLC cannot say which came first. The pessimistic reading is
//      the only one that cannot flatter the result, so the stop wins.
//  3 · BREAK-EVEN MAY NEVER WIDEN RISK. The move is applied only when it
//      IMPROVES the stop, and a stop-out afterwards is reported as a BREAK-EVEN
//      stop rather than a loss, because that is what it is.
//
// ONE TRADE AT A TIME. A new signal REPLACES whatever was being tracked; the
// superseded trade simply stops being followed. The engine has never held more
// than one position per side and this module does not pretend otherwise.
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
type Trade
    int   dir   = 0        // 0 = nothing tracked
    float entry = na
    float sl    = na       // the LIVE stop — this is what moves to break-even
    float tp1   = na
    float tp2   = na
    float tp3   = na
    int   bar   = na       // the signal bar, excluded from evaluation (rule 1)
    int   hit   = 0        // highest rung reached: 0 none · 1 · 2 · 3
    bool  be    = false
    int   res   = 0        // 0 live · 1 stopped · 2 break-even stop · 3 TP3

var Trade trd = Trade.new()
// this bar's events — read by the marks (MODULE 17), the dashboard and the alerts
int  tdTp   = 0            // the rung newly reached on THIS bar, 0 = none
bool tdSl   = false
bool tdBe   = false

if trackTrade and confirmed
    // ---- 1 · manage what is already being tracked, on bars AFTER the entry bar
    if trd.dir != 0 and bar_index > trd.bar
        bool  isL = trd.dir == 1
        if isL ? low <= trd.sl : high >= trd.sl
            trd.res := trd.be ? 2 : 1      // rule 2 · the stop wins a shared candle
            trd.dir := 0
            tdSl    := true
        else
            // the ladder is monotone by construction (f_tp caps t1 and promotes t3),
            // so testing it in order can only ever raise the rung
            int rung = trd.hit
            if isL ? high >= trd.tp1 : low <= trd.tp1
                rung := 1
            if isL ? high >= trd.tp2 : low <= trd.tp2
                rung := 2
            if isL ? high >= trd.tp3 : low <= trd.tp3
                rung := 3
            if rung > trd.hit
                trd.hit := rung
                tdTp    := rung
            if trd.hit >= 3
                trd.res := 3
                trd.dir := 0
            else if not trd.be and beAfter != "Off" and trd.hit >= (beAfter == "TP1" ? 1 : 2)
                float nsl = isL ? trd.entry + beOffset * atr : trd.entry - beOffset * atr
                if isL ? nsl > trd.sl : nsl < trd.sl     // rule 3 · never widen risk
                    trd.sl := nsl
                    trd.be := true
                    tdBe   := true
    // ---- 2 · adopt this bar's signal, AFTER the management pass, so one candle
    //          may both close the previous trade and open the next one
    if sigDir != 0
        trd.dir   := sigDir
        trd.entry := sigEntry
        trd.sl    := sigSL
        trd.tp1   := sigTP1
        trd.tp2   := sigTP2
        trd.tp3   := sigTP3
        trd.bar   := bar_index
        trd.hit   := 0
        trd.be    := false
        trd.res   := 0

f_trdTxt() =>
    trd.dir != 0 ? (trd.dir == 1 ? "LONG " : "SHORT ") + (trd.hit >= 2 ? "TP2" : trd.hit == 1 ? "TP1" : "open") + (trd.be ? " · BE" : "") :
         trd.res == 1 ? "stopped" : trd.res == 2 ? "break-even stop" : trd.res == 3 ? "TP3 ✓" : "—"

// ═══════════ MODULE 16B · ICT DETECTION + MARKING — REMOVED IN v5.8 ═══════════
// v5.8 compiled to 101 135 / 100 256 tokens (CE10117). Per the header's cut
// order, ICT-CUT-1/2/3 (the whole inline ICT module: inputs, IctM, f_ict) were
// deleted. The FULL ICT layer runs standalone as 10.ict-forensic-layer-v1.pine
// alongside this script — nothing is lost, and v5.7's inline module was the
// budget subset of that file anyway.
// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

// ══════════════════════════════════════════ MODULE 17 · VISUAL ENGINE
// Everything v2 drew is still drawn here. The one thing that changed is
// authority: no drawing in this module decides anything, and the only entry
// mark on the chart is the one the TRADE ENGINE emitted (Phase 28).
// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// ---- key level plots ---------------------------------------------------------
plot(showLevels ? lastRes : na, "Resistance (last swing high)",
     color = color.new(COL_BEAR, 50), style = plot.style_linebr)
plot(showLevels ? lastSup : na, "Support (last swing low)",
     color = color.new(COL_BULL, 50), style = plot.style_linebr)
plot(showStruct and showProt ? protLowE : na, "Protected low (external)",
     color = color.new(COL_BULL, 15), style = plot.style_linebr, linewidth = 2)
plot(showStruct and showProt ? protHighE : na, "Protected high (external)",
     color = color.new(COL_BEAR, 15), style = plot.style_linebr, linewidth = 2)

// ---- liquidity / structure marks (confirmed bars only — no intrabar flicker)
// V7.6 · F-15 · the ◆ marks were a LEVEL test, not an EVENT test, so they re-printed
//   on every bar that wicked under the same swing low and closed back above it. One
//   mark per level now, latched on the level itself — display only, nothing reads these.
var float swpLoSeen = na
var float swpHiSeen = na
swpLoNew = showSweeps and confirmed and not na(lastSup) and low < lastSup and close > lastSup and (na(swpLoSeen) or swpLoSeen != lastSup)
swpHiNew = showSweeps and confirmed and not na(lastRes) and high > lastRes and close < lastRes and (na(swpHiSeen) or swpHiSeen != lastRes)
if swpLoNew
    swpLoSeen := lastSup
if swpHiNew
    swpHiSeen := lastRes
plotchar(swpLoNew, "Sell-side sweep", "◆", location.belowbar,
     color.new(COL_BULL, 0), size = size.tiny)
plotchar(swpHiNew, "Buy-side sweep", "◆", location.abovebar,
     color.new(COL_BEAR, 0), size = size.tiny)
plotchar(showSweeps and eqlSwept, "EQL pool raided", "◈", location.belowbar,
     color.new(COL_BULL, 0), size = size.tiny)
plotchar(showSweeps and eqhSwept, "EQH pool raided", "◈", location.abovebar,
     color.new(COL_BEAR, 0), size = size.tiny)
plotchar(showSesHL and sesHiRaid, "Session high raided", "▿", location.abovebar,
     color.new(COL_BEAR, 15), size = size.tiny)
plotchar(showSesHL and sesLoRaid, "Session low raided", "▵", location.belowbar,
     color.new(COL_BULL, 15), size = size.tiny)
plotchar(showSesHL and sesHiBroke, "Session high broken", "⇡", location.abovebar,
     color.new(COL_BULL, 15), size = size.tiny)
plotchar(showSesHL and sesLoBroke, "Session low broken", "⇣", location.belowbar,
     color.new(COL_BEAR, 15), size = size.tiny)
// V5.6 · EXTERNAL liquidity raided (PDH / PDL / PWH / PWL / external swing).
// v5.5 marked swing sweeps, EQ pool raids and session raids but had no mark at
// all for the highest-priority liquidity on the chart.
plotchar(lqExtHiMark, "External BSL raided", "◇", location.abovebar,
     color.new(COL_BEAR, 0), size = size.tiny)
plotchar(lqExtLoMark, "External SSL raided", "◇", location.belowbar,
     color.new(COL_BULL, 0), size = size.tiny)
// the ONLY entry marks on the chart
plotchar(finalLong,  "TRADE ENGINE · LONG",  "▲", location.belowbar, COL_BULL, size = size.small)
plotchar(finalShort, "TRADE ENGINE · SHORT", "▼", location.abovebar, COL_BEAR, size = size.small)

// ---- session / volatility shading -------------------------------------------
bgcolor(paintKZ and timeframe.isintraday and inAsia ? color.new(color.purple, 95) : na, title = "Asia KZ")
bgcolor(paintKZ and timeframe.isintraday and inLon  ? color.new(color.blue,   94) : na, title = "London KZ")
bgcolor(paintKZ and timeframe.isintraday and inNY   ? color.new(color.orange, 94) : na, title = "NY AM KZ")
bgcolor(paintKZ and timeframe.isintraday and inSB   ? color.new(color.teal,   92) : na, title = "Silver Bullet KZ")
bgcolor(paintNews and timeframe.isintraday and inVol ? color.new(color.red,   90) : na, title = "Scheduled volatility window")

// ---- the single entry label --------------------------------------------------
// §42 · the label answers WHY: grade, engine, POI tier, raid grade, the session
// the trade belongs to, and then the full causal line from f_reason.
sesTag = not timeframe.isintraday ? "" : inSB ? " · SB" : inNY ? " · NY" : inLon ? " · LONDON" : inAsia ? " · ASIA" : ""
f_entryLbl(bool isL, int g, int sc, float rr, string tag, int tier, int rq, bool rev, string reason) =>
    string t = f_grTxt(g) + (isL ? " LONG " : " SHORT ") + str.tostring(sc) + " · " + str.tostring(rr, "#.#") + "R\n" + tag + " · " +
         f_tierTxt(tier) + " · raid " + f_rqTxt(rq) + (rev ? " · REV" : "") + sesTag
    if showReason
        t := t + "\n" + reason
    f_chip(isL, t, color.new(isL ? COL_BULL : COL_BEAR, uiTrans), color.white, sigSize)
if finalLong
    f_entryLbl(true, gradeL, scoreLong, rrL, tagL, tierL, rqL, revL, reasonLong)
if finalShort
    f_entryLbl(false, gradeS, scoreShort, rrS, tagS, tierS, rqS, revS, reasonShort)

// ---- V7.10 · CUT 6 · the developing-setup ⋯ preview is gone. It changed no state
//      and raised no alert; the debug panel reports the same stage, on the same bar.

// ---- entry / SL / TP of the last emitted signal ------------------------------
// V5.6-L12 · five identical line + label pairs, held in two arrays and drawn by
// one helper. Same colours, same styles, same text as the ten named objects.
var tradeLns = array.new<line>()
var tradeLbs = array.new<label>()
f_tradeLvl(int x2, float y, color lc, string txt, color bg) =>
    array.push(tradeLns, line.new(bar_index, y, x2, y, color = lc, style = line.style_dashed))
    array.push(tradeLbs, label.new(x2, y, txt, style = label.style_label_left, color = bg, textcolor = color.white, size = infoSize))
    true

if showTrade and sigDir != 0
    while array.size(tradeLns) > 0
        line.delete(array.pop(tradeLns))
    while array.size(tradeLbs) > 0
        label.delete(array.pop(tradeLbs))
    int x2 = bar_index + TRADE_BARS
    color tpc = sigDir == 1 ? COL_BULL : COL_BEAR
    f_tradeLvl(x2, sigEntry, color.gray,          "E " + str.tostring(sigEntry, format.mintick), color.new(color.gray, 20))
    f_tradeLvl(x2, sigSL,    COL_BEAR,            "SL " + str.tostring(sigSL, format.mintick) + " · " + f_slTxt(sigSlSrc), color.new(COL_BEAR, 20))
    f_tradeLvl(x2, sigTP1,   color.new(tpc, 45),  "TP1 " + str.tostring(sigTP1, format.mintick), color.new(tpc, 45))
    f_tradeLvl(x2, sigTP2,   color.new(tpc, 20),  "TP2 " + str.tostring(sigTP2, format.mintick), color.new(tpc, 30))
    f_tradeLvl(x2, sigTP3,   tpc,                 "TP3 " + str.tostring(sigTP3, format.mintick), color.new(tpc, 15))

// V7.9 · CUT 4 · the v7.8 on-chart TP / SL / BE chips are gone. The five [TRADE]
//   alerts still fire and dashboard row 17 still reports the state — only the
//   marks go, and with them `tdDirE`, which nothing else read.
// V7.9 · CUT 3 · the drawn SL no longer follows the stop to break-even (the stop
//   itself still moves, the dashboard still says BE and the alert still fires).

// ---- live HTF FVG POI boxes + the machine's LOCKED execution FVG (state 7) ----
// V7.5 · RECLAIM R3 · four near-identical box.new calls → one helper.
f_ctxBox(int x1, float top, float bot, color c, string txt, bool dashed) =>
    box.new(x1, top, bar_index + TRADE_BARS, bot, border_color = color.new(c, dashed ? 50 : 35),
         bgcolor = color.new(c, dashed ? 95 : 92), border_style = dashed ? line.style_dashed : line.style_solid,
         text = txt, text_size = size.small, text_color = color.new(c, 0), text_halign = text.align_right)
var box hPoiBuyBx  = na
var box hPoiSellBx = na
var box machFvgBxL = na
var box machFvgBxS = na
if barstate.islast
    box.delete(hPoiBuyBx)
    box.delete(hPoiSellBx)
    box.delete(machFvgBxL)
    box.delete(machFvgBxS)
    if showPoi and not na(hPoiBTop)
        hPoiBuyBx := f_ctxBox(bar_index - 10, hPoiBTop, hPoiBBot, COL_BULL, "HTF " + htfTf + " demand POI" + (nz(hPoiBIn) == 0 ? " · fresh" : " · tested ×" + str.tostring(nz(hPoiBIn))), true)
    if showPoi and not na(hPoiSTop)
        hPoiSellBx := f_ctxBox(bar_index - 10, hPoiSTop, hPoiSBot, COL_BEAR, "HTF " + htfTf + " supply POI" + (nz(hPoiSIn) == 0 ? " · fresh" : " · tested ×" + str.tostring(nz(hPoiSIn))), true)
    if upS.st == ST_FVG and not na(upS.fvgTop)
        machFvgBxL := f_ctxBox(upS.fvgBar, upS.fvgTop, upS.fvgBot, COL_BULL, "◎ FVG · retest", false)
    if dnS.st == ST_FVG and not na(dnS.fvgTop)
        machFvgBxS := f_ctxBox(dnS.fvgBar, dnS.fvgTop, dnS.fvgBot, COL_BEAR, "◎ FVG · retest", false)

// ---- V7.10 · CUT 7 · the ◂ target BSL / SSL labels are gone. The dashboard's
//      "Liquidity" row prints the same two ranked levels with their tags, and the
//      pool lines themselves are still drawn by MODULE 7.

// ---- V7.10 · CUT 8 · the premium / discount shading and the dealing-range EQ +
//      OTE box are gone. They were pictures of two numbers the dashboard already
//      prints ("PD" row: HTF and leg position as a percentage). The ENGINE is
//      untouched: rangeOK, eqLvl, pdPos, premOK / discOK, inDiscExt / inPremExt and
//      the OTE bands are all computed in MODULE 8 and still gate and score exactly
//      as before.


// ---- extend live zone drawings ONCE, on the last bar -------------------------
if barstate.islast and array.size(zones) > 0
    for i = 0 to array.size(zones) - 1
        Zone z = array.get(zones, i)
        box.set_right(z.bx, bar_index + 1)
        line.set_x2(z.mid, bar_index + 1)

// ---- NEXT buy / sell POI labels + highlight ---------------------------------
var label nextBuyLb  = na
var label nextSellLb = na
if barstate.islast
    label.delete(nextBuyLb)
    label.delete(nextSellLb)
    if showNext and array.size(zones) > 0
        for i = 0 to array.size(zones) - 1
            Zone z = array.get(zones, i)
            float t = box.get_top(z.bx)
            float b = box.get_bottom(z.bx)
            bool isNext = (not z.isSupply and t == nbTop and b == nbBot) or (z.isSupply and t == nsTop and b == nsBot)
            box.set_border_width(z.bx, isNext ? 2 : 1)
    if showNext and not na(nbTop)
        string nbDist = close <= nbTop ? "inside" : str.tostring((close - nbTop) / close * 100, "#.##") + "% below"
        nextBuyLb := label.new(bar_index + TRADE_BARS, (nbTop + nbBot) / 2,
             "NEXT BUY " + f_tierTxt(nbTier) + " ▸ " + str.tostring(nbBot, format.mintick) + "–" + str.tostring(nbTop, format.mintick) + " (" + nbDist + ")",
             style = label.style_label_left, color = color.new(COL_BULL, 40),
             textcolor = color.white, size = infoSize)
    if showNext and not na(nsBot)
        string nsDist = close >= nsBot ? "inside" : str.tostring((nsBot - close) / close * 100, "#.##") + "% above"
        nextSellLb := label.new(bar_index + TRADE_BARS, (nsTop + nsBot) / 2,
             "NEXT SELL " + f_tierTxt(nsTier) + " ▸ " + str.tostring(nsBot, format.mintick) + "–" + str.tostring(nsTop, format.mintick) + " (" + nsDist + ")",
             style = label.style_label_left, color = color.new(COL_BEAR, 40),
             textcolor = color.white, size = infoSize)

// ══════════════════════════════════════════════ MODULE 18 · DASHBOARD
// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
f_biasCol(int b) => b > 0 ? color.new(COL_BULL, 75) : b < 0 ? color.new(COL_BEAR, 75) : color.new(color.gray, 75)
f_stTxt(int st) =>
    st == ST_NONE ? "0 idle" : st == ST_CTX ? "1 HTF ctx" : st == ST_TGT ? "2 target" :
         st == ST_RAID ? "3 raided" : st == ST_POI ? "4 POI" : st == ST_MSS ? "5 MSS" :
         st == ST_DISP ? "6 disp" : st == ST_FVG ? "7 FVG" : st == ST_ENTRY ? "8 ENTRY" : "9 spent"
f_prodTxt(int p) =>
    p == PR_FEM ? "FEM" : p == PR_MACH ? "Sweep+POI+MSS" : p == PR_SD ? "S/D retest" :
         p == PR_TRAP ? "Trap engine" : "—"
// V7.5 · §33 · the OWNED chain of a setup as it stands, one line per side
f_narrTxt(Setup s) =>
    s.st < ST_RAID ? "—" : "raid " + f_rqTxt(s.liqQual) + (s.st >= ST_POI ? " · POI " + f_tierTxt(s.poiTier) : "") +
         (s.st >= ST_MSS ? (s.mssChoch ? " · CHoCH" : " · CISD") : "") + (s.st >= ST_DISP ? " · disp q" + str.tostring(s.dispQ) : "") +
         (s.st >= ST_FVG ? " · FVG ✓ · OTE " + str.tostring(s.oteQ) : "")

var table dash = table.new(position.top_right, 2, 18,
     border_color = color.new(color.gray, 70), border_width = 1)
hdrBg  = color.new(color.gray, 60)
rowBg  = color.new(color.gray, 85)

// V5.6-L12 · one dashboard row = label cell + value cell. Every row below used
// to spell both table.cell calls out in full; same cells, same arguments.
f_dRow(int r, string lbl, string val, color bg, string sz, string tip) =>
    table.cell(dash, 0, r, lbl, text_size = dashSize, bgcolor = rowBg, text_color = color.white, tooltip = tip)
    table.cell(dash, 1, r, val, bgcolor = bg, text_color = color.white, text_size = sz)
    true

if showDash and barstate.islast
    int livePools = 0
    if array.size(pools) > 0
        for i = 0 to array.size(pools) - 1
            Pool p = array.get(pools, i)
            if p.state <= PS_VAL
                livePools += 1
    table.cell(dash, 0, 0, "ICT-TE v7.10", bgcolor = hdrBg, text_color = color.white, text_size = dashSize)
    table.cell(dash, 1, 0, "context · setup", bgcolor = hdrBg, text_color = color.white, text_size = dashSize)
    // V7.5 · §33 · hierarchy first: HTF state and its two layers, then the regime
    f_dRow(1, "HTF " + htfTf, f_hsTxt(htfState) + " · mom " + f_biasTxt(htfMom) + " · struct " + f_biasTxt(hStrDir),
         htfConf ? color.new(color.orange, 60) : f_biasCol(htfDir), dashSize2,
         "§8 / §9 · HTF STATE = the HTF's external STRUCTURE (its own confirmed pivots) read first, the context timeframe's EMA momentum second. Agree → BULL / BEAR. Disagree → CONFLICT, handled by the HTF-conflict setting (Off / Downgrade / A+ only / Block), never silently resolved. No HTF structure yet → momentum alone decides; NEUTRAL only while neither is known.")
    f_dRow(2, "Bias " + biasFilter + " · PD", f_biasTxt(filtBias) + (na(htfPd) ? "" : " · HTF " + (htfPd >= 0.5 ? "prem " : "disc ") + str.tostring(htfPd * 100, "#") + "%") + (na(pdPos) ? "" : " · leg " + (pdPos >= 0.5 ? "prem " : "disc ") + str.tostring(pdPos * 100, "#") + "%"),
         f_biasCol(filtBias), dashSize2,
         "LEFT: the optional bias FILTER timeframe. RIGHT: where price sits in the HTF dealing range and in the chart's impulse-leg range (protected swing → structural extreme, §23).")
    // V7.7 · THE LADDER ABOVE THIS CHART, restored (display only). Reading order is
    //   fast → slow, so a glance answers "am I trading with the higher rungs or
    //   against them?". The count on the right is how many USABLE rungs agree; a
    //   rung at or below the chart timeframe prints · and is excluded from it.
    f_dRow(3, "MTF 5·15·1H·4H", "5m " + f_mtfArrow(mtfU5, mtfB5) + "  15m " + f_mtfArrow(mtfU15, mtfB15) + "  1H " + f_mtfArrow(mtfU60, mtfB60) + "  4H " + f_mtfArrow(mtfU240, mtfB240) + "   " + (mtfNet > 0 ? "+" : "") + str.tostring(mtfNet) + "/" + str.tostring(mtfN),
         mtfN > 0 and mtfNet == mtfN ? color.new(COL_BULL, 55) : mtfN > 0 and mtfNet == -mtfN ? color.new(COL_BEAR, 55) : rowBg, dashSize2,
         "The EMA-momentum bias of the 5m / 15m / 1H / 4H candles, each read from the LAST CLOSED candle of that timeframe (expr[1] + lookahead_on — non-repainting, the same construction every HTF value in this file uses).\nDISPLAY ONLY: no gate, grade, score, tier, stop, target or state transition reads it. The counter-trend bias FILTER is a separate setting with its own read, so this row can never change a signal.\nThe figure on the right is the NET of the usable rungs (+2/4 = two more bull than bear) — it reaches +N or −N only when every usable rung agrees.\nA rung at or below the chart timeframe shows · and is left out of both numbers.")
    f_dRow(4, "Regime · AMD", f_rgTxt(regime) + " · " + amdTxt + " · chop " + str.tostring(congN) + "/4" + (congested ? " ⚠" : ""),
         congested ? color.new(color.orange, 65) : rowBg, dashSize2,
         "§36 / §39 / §24 · regime from price action (TREND / RANGE / EXPANSION / CHOP) and the execution policy it drives; the session's accumulation / manipulation / distribution phase; the congestion reading out of four.")
    f_dRow(5, "Liquidity", (na(tgtSell) ? "—" : str.tostring(tgtSell, format.mintick) + " " + tgtSellTag) + " / " +
         (na(tgtBuy) ? "—" : str.tostring(tgtBuy, format.mintick) + " " + tgtBuyTag) + "  (" + str.tostring(livePools) + ")", rowBg, dashSize2,
         "What price is hunting next: the ranked un-raided pool on each side (SSL / BSL), plus the number of live pools tracked.")
    string sweepTxt = (sslInPlay ? "SSL✓" + (sslStrong ? "★ " : " ") : "") + (bslInPlay ? "BSL✓" + (bslStrong ? "★" : "") : "")
    f_dRow(6, "Raid in play", sweepTxt == "" ? "none" : sweepTxt, rowBg, dashSize,
         "The freshest graded raid on each side (★ = strong rejection). Display only — a setup binds the pool it LOCKED, a zone the raid stamped on it, never this one.")
    string nbTxt = na(nbTop) ? "—" : str.tostring(nbBot, format.mintick) + "–" + str.tostring(nbTop, format.mintick) + " " + f_tierTxt(nbTier)
    string nsTxt = na(nsBot) ? "—" : str.tostring(nsBot, format.mintick) + "–" + str.tostring(nsTop, format.mintick) + " " + f_tierTxt(nsTier)
    f_dRow(7, "Next POI", "B " + nbTxt + " · S " + nsTxt, rowBg, dashSize2, "")
    string structTxt = "int " + (msDir > 0 ? "▲" : msDir < 0 ? "▼" : "—") + " · ext " + (extDir > 0 ? "▲" : extDir < 0 ? "▼" : "—")
    f_dRow(8, "Structure", structTxt, f_biasCol(extDir), dashSize, "")
    string kzTxt = not timeframe.isintraday ? "—" : inSB ? "Silver Bullet" : inNY ? "NY AM" : inLon ? "London" : inAsia ? "Asia" : "off-hours"
    f_dRow(9, "Session", kzTxt + (inVol and timeframe.isintraday ? " · ⚠ VOL WINDOW" : ""),
         inVol and timeframe.isintraday ? color.new(color.red, 60) : inSB ? color.new(color.teal, 60) : rowBg, dashSize,
         "Session context (a FILTER, never a signal) and the SCHEDULED volatility window — fixed clock slots, not a live economic calendar.")
    f_dRow(10, "◎ Machine", "L " + f_stTxt(upS.st) + " · S " + f_stTxt(dnS.st),
         upS.st >= ST_MSS ? color.new(COL_BULL, 60) : dnS.st >= ST_MSS ? color.new(COL_BEAR, 60) : rowBg, dashSize,
         "State machine per direction: 0 idle → 1 HTF context → 2 liquidity target → 3 raided → 4 POI → 5 MSS → 6 displacement → 7 FVG locked → 8 ENTRY.")
    f_dRow(11, "Narrative L", f_narrTxt(upS), upS.st >= ST_RAID ? color.new(COL_BULL, 80) : rowBg, dashSize2,
         "The OWNED chain of the long setup as it stands: raid grade · POI tier · MSS kind · displacement quality (0-6) · FVG · OTE band (1 prime · 2 deep · 3 beyond · 0 none).")
    f_dRow(12, "Narrative S", f_narrTxt(dnS), dnS.st >= ST_RAID ? color.new(COL_BEAR, 80) : rowBg, dashSize2, "Same, short side.")
    f_dRow(13, "Grade · RR · room", "L " + f_grTxt(gradeL) + " " + str.tostring(rrL, "#.#") + "R " + f_roomTxt(roomL) + "  ·  S " + f_grTxt(gradeS) + " " + str.tostring(rrS, "#.#") + "R " + f_roomTxt(roomS),
         gradeL == GR_AP ? color.new(COL_BULL, 45) : gradeS == GR_AP ? color.new(COL_BEAR, 45) : rowBg, dashSize2,
         "Grade of the CURRENT candidate on each side, its REALISTIC reward-to-risk (TP2 of the liquidity ladder, clamped by opposing liquidity) and the room to the nearest opposing pool / POI in R. RR is a HARD gate per grade.")
    f_dRow(14, "Event", "L " + (evIdL == 0 ? "—" : "#" + str.tostring(evIdL) + " " + evTagL + " " + f_rqTxt(rqL)) +
         "  ·  S " + (evIdS == 0 ? "—" : "#" + str.tostring(evIdS) + " " + evTagS + " " + f_rqTxt(rqS)), rowBg, dashSize2,
         "§7 · the identity of the physical liquidity event each candidate is built on: pool id, level, graded raid. ONE event, one id — every engine that recognises it is a DESCRIPTOR of it, never an extra confirmation.")
    f_dRow(15, "Run", run.dir == 0 ? "no run" : (run.dir == 1 ? "▲ " : "▼ ") + f_rnTxt(run.cls) + " " + str.tostring(run.legs) + " leg" + (run.intToExt ? " int→ext" : ""),
         run.cls == RN_CONT ? color.new(color.yellow, 75) : rowBg, dashSize2,
         "§11 · liquidity-run classification (none / 1 leg / RUN / exhausted / REVERSED). Fading a RUN costs one grade, or is blocked.")
    color scBg = aPlusLong ? color.new(COL_BULL, 45) : aPlusShort ? color.new(COL_BEAR, 45) : rowBg
    f_dRow(16, "Alignment", "L:" + str.tostring(scoreLong) + "  S:" + str.tostring(scoreShort) + (aPlusLong ? "  A+▲" : aPlusShort ? "  A+▼" : ""), scBg, dashSize,
         "Narrative alignment 0–100 — NOT a win probability, and SOFT: it ranks candidates that already passed every hard gate. Context 25 · Liquidity 25 · POI 20 · Trigger 20 · Execution 10, every ingredient from the setup's own owned events.")

    // V7.8 · what happened to the LAST signal. The engine's own record, so the
    //   dashboard finally answers "and then what?" as well as "why?".
    f_dRow(17, "Trade", f_trdTxt(),
         trd.dir == 1 ? color.new(COL_BULL, 60) : trd.dir == -1 ? color.new(COL_BEAR, 60) : trd.res == 1 ? color.new(COL_BEAR, 75) : trd.res == 3 ? color.new(COL_BULL, 75) : rowBg, dashSize2,
         "BOOKKEEPING on the last emitted signal, and nothing more — no order, no sizing, and no path back into any gate, grade, score or state machine.\nWithin a single candle the order of touches is unknowable from OHLC, so the STOP is always assumed first; the signal bar itself is never evaluated.\nA new signal replaces whatever was being tracked.")

// ═══════════════════════════════════════ MODULE 19 · DEBUG PANEL (§27)
// Rows 1–9 are the state machine per direction. With verbose debug on, rows
// 10–19 print the OWNERSHIP trace — setup id, which pool was locked as the
// target, which pool was actually raided and how well, which POI, which shift,
// which expansion, which gap, and every one of their bars — followed by the
// narrative verdict, the grade and the risk numbers. The last row is the exact
// gate that stopped the trade. Between them they answer "why did nothing
// print?" and "why did THAT print?" without guesswork.
// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
dbgPosition = dbgPos == "Bottom right" ? position.bottom_right : dbgPos == "Bottom left" ? position.bottom_left :
     dbgPos == "Middle right" ? position.middle_right : position.top_left
var table dbg = table.new(dbgPosition, 3, 25, border_color = color.new(color.gray, 70), border_width = 1)

f_dbgTxt(int r, string lbl, string lTxt, string sTxt) =>
    table.cell(dbg, 0, r, lbl, text_size = dbgSize, bgcolor = rowBg, text_color = color.white, text_halign = text.align_left)
    table.cell(dbg, 1, r, lTxt, text_size = dbgSize, bgcolor = rowBg, text_color = color.new(color.white, 10), text_halign = text.align_left)
    table.cell(dbg, 2, r, sTxt, text_size = dbgSize, bgcolor = rowBg, text_color = color.new(color.white, 10), text_halign = text.align_left)
    true

f_barTxt(int b) => na(b) ? "—" : str.tostring(bar_index - b) + " ago"

f_dbgRow(int r, string lbl, bool okL, bool okS) =>
    table.cell(dbg, 0, r, lbl, text_size = dbgSize, bgcolor = rowBg, text_color = color.white, text_halign = text.align_left)
    table.cell(dbg, 1, r, f_tick(okL), text_size = dbgSize, text_color = okL ? COL_BULL : color.new(color.white, 40),
         bgcolor = okL ? color.new(COL_BULL, 82) : rowBg)
    table.cell(dbg, 2, r, f_tick(okS), text_size = dbgSize, text_color = okS ? COL_BEAR : color.new(color.white, 40),
         bgcolor = okS ? color.new(COL_BEAR, 82) : rowBg)
    true

if showDebug and barstate.islast
    table.cell(dbg, 0, 0, "SETUP STATE", bgcolor = hdrBg, text_color = color.white, text_size = dbgSize)
    table.cell(dbg, 1, 0, "LONG",  bgcolor = color.new(COL_BULL, 55), text_color = color.white, text_size = dbgSize)
    table.cell(dbg, 2, 0, "SHORT", bgcolor = color.new(COL_BEAR, 55), text_color = color.white, text_size = dbgSize)
    // V7.6 · RECLAIM R2 · the nine state rows were NINE inlined copies of f_dbgRow
    //   (150 src each, ~1 050 of pure duplication) spelling one pattern, because the
    //   state constants ST_CTX … ST_ENTRY ARE 1 … 8: row r is ticked when st >= r.
    //   Row 9 is the emission, which is not a state. Same nine rows, same labels, same
    //   colours, one inlined copy. Display only — the lineage's rule is never to CUT
    //   the debug panel, and nothing here is cut.
    dbgLbl = array.from("1 HTF CONTEXT", "2 LIQ TARGET", "3 SWEEP/RAID", "4 POI",
         "5 MSS/CHoCH", "6 DISPLACEMENT", "7 FVG LOCKED", "8 RETEST/ENTRY", "9 EMITTED")
    for r = 1 to 9
        f_dbgRow(r, array.get(dbgLbl, r - 1), r == 9 ? finalLong : upS.st >= r, r == 9 ? finalShort : dnS.st >= r)
    if dbgVerbose
        f_dbgTxt(10, "setup id",  str.tostring(upS.id), str.tostring(dnS.id))
        f_dbgTxt(11, "target",    (upS.tgtId == 0 ? "—" : "#" + str.tostring(upS.tgtId) + " " + upS.tgtTag + " " + f_barTxt(upS.tgtBar)),
             (dnS.tgtId == 0 ? "—" : "#" + str.tostring(dnS.tgtId) + " " + dnS.tgtTag + " " + f_barTxt(dnS.tgtBar)))
        f_dbgTxt(12, "raid",      (upS.liqId == 0 ? "—" : "#" + str.tostring(upS.liqId) + " " + upS.liqTag + " " + f_rqTxt(upS.liqQual) + " " + f_barTxt(upS.liqBar)),
             (dnS.liqId == 0 ? "—" : "#" + str.tostring(dnS.liqId) + " " + dnS.liqTag + " " + f_rqTxt(dnS.liqQual) + " " + f_barTxt(dnS.liqBar)))
        f_dbgTxt(13, "POI · array", (na(upS.poiBar) ? "—" : (upS.poiIsHtf ? "HTF" : "#" + str.tostring(upS.poiId) + " " + f_paTxt(upS.poiPa)) + " " + f_tierTxt(upS.poiTier) + " " + f_barTxt(upS.poiBar)),
             (na(dnS.poiBar) ? "—" : (dnS.poiIsHtf ? "HTF" : "#" + str.tostring(dnS.poiId) + " " + f_paTxt(dnS.poiPa)) + " " + f_tierTxt(dnS.poiTier) + " " + f_barTxt(dnS.poiBar)))
        f_dbgTxt(14, "MSS",       (na(upS.mssBar) ? "—" : (upS.mssChoch ? "CHoCH " : "CISD ") + f_barTxt(upS.mssBar)),
             (na(dnS.mssBar) ? "—" : (dnS.mssChoch ? "CHoCH " : "CISD ") + f_barTxt(dnS.mssBar)))
        f_dbgTxt(15, "disp · quality", (na(upS.dispBar) ? "—" : str.tostring(nz(upS.dispBody), "#.##") + "×ATR q" + str.tostring(upS.dispQ) + "/6 " + f_barTxt(upS.dispBar)),
             (na(dnS.dispBar) ? "—" : str.tostring(nz(dnS.dispBody), "#.##") + "×ATR q" + str.tostring(dnS.dispQ) + "/6 " + f_barTxt(dnS.dispBar)))
        // §44 / §48 · the gap, its OTE verdict, the inducement and the timing
        f_dbgTxt(16, "FVG · OTE · IDM", (na(upS.fvgBar) ? "—" : str.tostring(nz(upS.fvgH) / atr, "#.##") + "×ATR " + f_barTxt(upS.fvgBar) + " · " + str.tostring(upS.oteQ) + "/" + str.tostring(upS.fvgTouches) + " · " + f_tick(upS.induced) + " · " + f_tmTxt(upS.st < ST_FVG ? TM_PREM : upS.fvgTouch >= 0 and bar_index - upS.fvgTouch > lateBars ? TM_LATE : TM_VALID)),
             (na(dnS.fvgBar) ? "—" : str.tostring(nz(dnS.fvgH) / atr, "#.##") + "×ATR " + f_barTxt(dnS.fvgBar) + " · " + str.tostring(dnS.oteQ) + "/" + str.tostring(dnS.fvgTouches) + " · " + f_tick(dnS.induced) + " · " + f_tmTxt(dnS.st < ST_FVG ? TM_PREM : dnS.fvgTouch >= 0 and bar_index - dnS.fvgTouch > lateBars ? TM_LATE : TM_VALID)))
        f_dbgTxt(17, "narrative", (narrLong ? "✓ owned + in order" : "✗") + (upS.reversal ? " · REVERSAL" : ""),
             (narrShort ? "✓ owned + in order" : "✗") + (dnS.reversal ? " · REVERSAL" : ""))
        f_dbgTxt(18, "producer",  (prodL == 0 ? "—" : f_prodTxt(prodL) + " " + f_grTxt(gradeL) + " " + f_tierTxt(tierL) + " raid " + f_rqTxt(rqL)),
             (prodS == 0 ? "—" : f_prodTxt(prodS) + " " + f_grTxt(gradeS) + " " + f_tierTxt(tierS) + " raid " + f_rqTxt(rqS)))
        f_dbgTxt(19, "RR · room · score", str.tostring(rrL, "#.##") + "R · " + f_roomTxt(roomL) + " · " + str.tostring(scoreLong),
             str.tostring(rrS, "#.##") + "R · " + f_roomTxt(roomS) + " · " + str.tostring(scoreShort))
        // V5.5-E · the descriptor trace: which engines called this ONE event, and
        // what the capped allowance paid for all of them together
        f_dbgTxt(20, "event · desc",
             (evIdL == 0 ? "—" : "#" + str.tostring(evIdL) + " " + (descTL == "" ? "—" : descTL) + " D" + str.tostring(evL)),
             (evIdS == 0 ? "—" : "#" + str.tostring(evIdS) + " " + (descTS == "" ? "—" : descTS) + " D" + str.tostring(evS)))
        f_dbgTxt(21, "run · chop",
             (runFadeLong  ? "✗ fades " : "") + f_rnTxt(run.cls) + " " + str.tostring(run.legs) + "L · " + str.tostring(congN) + "/4",
             (runFadeShort ? "✗ fades " : "") + f_rnTxt(run.cls) + " " + str.tostring(run.legs) + "L · " + str.tostring(congN) + "/4")
        // V7.5 · §32 · the HTF state with both layers, the regime, and the STORY
        //   key the dedupe registry sees (dir | pool | raid bar | POI | MSS | FVG)
        f_dbgTxt(22, "HTF · regime", f_hsTxt(htfState) + " · mom " + f_biasTxt(htfMom) + " · struct " + f_biasTxt(hStrDir),
             f_rgTxt(regime) + " · " + amdTxt + (regDownL or regDownS ? " · policy ↓" : ""))
        f_dbgTxt(23, "story", storyL == "" ? "—" : storyL, storyS == "" ? "—" : storyS)
    table.cell(dbg, 0, 24, "BLOCKED", text_size = dbgSize, bgcolor = hdrBg, text_color = color.white, text_halign = text.align_left)
    table.cell(dbg, 1, 24, blockLong  == "" ? "—" : blockLong,  text_size = dbgSize, bgcolor = rowBg,
         text_color = color.new(color.white, 10), text_halign = text.align_left)
    table.cell(dbg, 2, 24, blockShort == "" ? "—" : blockShort, text_size = dbgSize, bgcolor = rowBg,
         text_color = color.new(color.white, 10), text_halign = text.align_left)

// ══════════════════════════════════════════════════ MODULE 20 · ALERTS
// Every v2 alert NAME is preserved so existing alerts keep working, but the
// [SIGNAL] conditions now come from the TRADE ENGINE, so one bar can no longer
// fire eleven of them at once (BUG-14). Names carry the decision layer:
//   [SIGNAL] an entry was emitted · [TRIG] structure / delivery event
//   [LIQ] liquidity taken · [POI] a POI / machine state changed
//   [CTX] context window. Use "Once per bar close".
// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// machine-state edges need a shadow of last bar's state (a UDT field has no
// history operator), updated at the very end of the script
var int upStPrev = 0
var int dnStPrev = 0
// v7.3 · the nine single-use aliases that stood here are inlined into their one
// alertcondition each (the reclaim class v7.2 proved on bosBull / mssUp / rrBn):
// same conditions, same alert names, same messages, nine fewer named globals.

// -- [SIGNAL] entries (the TRADE ENGINE is the only source)
alertcondition(finalLong and aPlusLong,        "[SIGNAL] A+ LONG (all 5 layers)",  "A+ LONG — HTF context → liquidity target → raid → POI → MSS → displacement → FVG → retest on {{ticker}} {{interval}}")
alertcondition(finalShort and aPlusShort,      "[SIGNAL] A+ SHORT (all 5 layers)", "A+ SHORT — HTF context → liquidity target → raid → POI → MSS → displacement → FVG → retest on {{ticker}} {{interval}}")
alertcondition(finalLong and gradeL == GR_A,    "[SIGNAL] A LONG",                  "A LONG — owned narrative, graded raid, PD array, MSS, displacement, FVG retest on {{ticker}} {{interval}}")
alertcondition(finalShort and gradeS == GR_A,  "[SIGNAL] A SHORT",                 "A SHORT — owned narrative, graded raid, PD array, MSS, displacement, FVG retest on {{ticker}} {{interval}}")
alertcondition(finalLong and scoreLong >= 80,  "[SIGNAL] ★ 80+ alignment LONG",    "High-alignment (80+) LONG on {{ticker}} {{interval}} — alignment, NOT win probability")
alertcondition(finalShort and scoreShort >= 80, "[SIGNAL] ★ 80+ alignment SHORT",  "High-alignment (80+) SHORT on {{ticker}} {{interval}} — alignment, NOT win probability")
alertcondition(finalLong,                      "[SIGNAL] Any LONG",                "LONG signal on {{ticker}} {{interval}}")
alertcondition(finalShort,                     "[SIGNAL] Any SHORT",               "SHORT signal on {{ticker}} {{interval}}")
alertcondition((finalLong and prodL == PR_FEM) or (finalShort and prodS == PR_FEM), "[SIGNAL] ◎ Fractal entry model",   "FEM executed (HTF POI → raid → LTF MSS → displacement → LTF FVG retest) on {{ticker}} {{interval}}")
alertcondition((finalLong and prodL == PR_MACH) or (finalShort and prodS == PR_MACH), "[SIGNAL] ★ Sweep + Zone entry",    "Sweep + POI + MSS narrative entry on {{ticker}} {{interval}}")
alertcondition((finalLong and prodL == PR_SD) or (finalShort and prodS == PR_SD), "[SIGNAL] S/D zone 50% entry",      "Supply/Demand 50% retest entry on {{ticker}} {{interval}}")
alertcondition((finalLong and prodL == PR_TRAP) or (finalShort and prodS == PR_TRAP), "[SIGNAL] ① Breakout Rejection Trap", "① Breakout Rejection Trap entry on {{ticker}} {{interval}}")
// -- [TRIG] structure / delivery events (information, not entries)
alertcondition(eChochUp or eChochDn,           "[TRIG] External CHoCH",            "EXTERNAL CHoCH — protected swing broken with displacement on {{ticker}} {{interval}}")
alertcondition(eBosUp or eBosDn,               "[TRIG] External BOS",              "EXTERNAL BOS on {{ticker}} {{interval}}")
alertcondition(chochUp or chochDn,               "[TRIG] Internal CHoCH",            "Internal CHoCH — the protected swing broke with displacement on {{ticker}} {{interval}}")
alertcondition(bosUp or bosDn,                 "[TRIG] Internal BOS",              "Internal BOS — trend continuation break on {{ticker}} {{interval}}")
alertcondition(cisdUp or cisdDn,                "[TRIG] CISD",                      "Change in state of delivery — order-flow flip, NOT a structure shift on {{ticker}} {{interval}}")
alertcondition(mssBull or mssBear,             "[TRIG] MSS",                       "MSS — a CHoCH, or a CISD carrying displacement on {{ticker}} {{interval}}")
alertcondition((ifcBull or ifcBear) and confirmed, "[TRIG] IFC candle",            "IFC candle (pool taken + displacement close) on {{ticker}} {{interval}}")
// -- V5.5-E · the new lifecycle engines. STATE TRANSITIONS, one per event (§35)
alertcondition(pa1Long or pa1Short,            "[TRIG] Trap confirmed",            "TRAP CONFIRMED — obvious liquidity broken, breakout failed, level reclaimed on {{ticker}} {{interval}}")
alertcondition(runFresh and run.cls == RN_CONT, "[LIQ] Liquidity run",             "LIQUIDITY RUN — a second liquidity pool broken in the same direction on {{ticker}} {{interval}}; fading it is gated until it exhausts")
// -- [LIQ] liquidity events
alertcondition(eqhSwept or eqlSwept,           "[LIQ] EQH/EQL pool swept",         "Equal highs/lows pool RAIDED on {{ticker}} {{interval}}")
alertcondition(sesHiRaid or sesLoRaid,         "[LIQ] Session high/low raided",    "Session level RAIDED (taken, closed back inside) on {{ticker}} {{interval}}")
alertcondition(sesHiBroke or sesLoBroke,       "[LIQ] Session high/low broken",    "Session level BROKEN (close beyond — expansion) on {{ticker}} {{interval}}")
alertcondition(poolRaids > 0,                  "[LIQ] Liquidity pool raided",      "A tracked liquidity pool was RAIDED on {{ticker}} {{interval}}")
// -- [POI] POI / machine state
alertcondition(touchNextBuy,                   "[POI] Price entered next BUY zone",  "Price entered the next BUY (demand) POI on {{ticker}} {{interval}}")
alertcondition(touchNextSell,                  "[POI] Price entered next SELL zone", "Price entered the next SELL (supply) POI on {{ticker}} {{interval}}")
alertcondition((upS.st == ST_POI and upStPrev != ST_POI) or (dnS.st == ST_POI and dnStPrev != ST_POI), "[POI] FEM: HTF POI tapped",        "Setup reached state 4 — POI confirmed at the raid, awaiting the LTF MSS on {{ticker}} {{interval}}")
alertcondition((upS.st == ST_MSS and upStPrev != ST_MSS) or (dnS.st == ST_MSS and dnStPrev != ST_MSS), "[POI] FEM: LTF shift confirmed",   "Setup reached state 5 — LTF MSS / CHoCH confirmed, awaiting displacement on {{ticker}} {{interval}}")
alertcondition((upS.st == ST_FVG and upStPrev != ST_FVG) or (dnS.st == ST_FVG and dnStPrev != ST_FVG), "[POI] FEM: LTF FVG locked",        "Setup reached state 7 — execution FVG locked, awaiting its retest on {{ticker}} {{interval}}")
// -- V7.8 · [TRADE] what happened AFTER the entry. These are the only alerts in
//    the file that are not about finding a trade, and they carry no opinion:
//    a rung was reached, or the stop was. See MODULE 16C for the two rules that
//    keep them honest (the signal bar is never evaluated; the stop wins a candle
//    that contains both).
alertcondition(tdTp == 1, "[TRADE] TP1 reached",  "TP1 reached on {{ticker}} {{interval}} — the tracked trade's first target")
alertcondition(tdTp == 2, "[TRADE] TP2 reached",  "TP2 reached on {{ticker}} {{interval}} — the rung RR was measured to")
alertcondition(tdTp == 3, "[TRADE] TP3 reached",  "TP3 reached on {{ticker}} {{interval}} — the tracked trade is complete")
alertcondition(tdSl,      "[TRADE] Stop hit",     "Stop hit on {{ticker}} {{interval}} — break-even if the stop had already been moved")
alertcondition(tdBe,      "[TRADE] Break-even",   "Stop moved to break-even on {{ticker}} {{interval}}")
// -- [CTX] context windows
alertcondition(inVol and not inVol[1],         "[CTX] Volatility window started",  "Scheduled volatility window started on {{ticker}} — entries suppressed")
alertcondition(not inVol and inVol[1],         "[CTX] Volatility window ended",    "Scheduled volatility window ended on {{ticker}} — entries re-enabled")

// shadow update: LAST statement in the script, after every consumer above
if confirmed
    upStPrev := upS.st
    dnStPrev := dnS.st
